Motivated by this limitation, we propose Fused Reference Alignment Prediction (FRAP), which leverages the complementary strengths of an external foundation model and the base model to construct a more reliable surrogate of the ground-truth labels. FRAP aligns the prediction distribution of the foundation model with that of the base model by applying temperature-scaled calibration that minimizes their divergence. The aligned predictions are fused through confidence-based weighting into a refined reference distribution that integrates robustness from the foundation model and domain-specific expertise from the base model, and performance estimation is obtained by measuring how closely the base model predictions agree with this reference. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and architectures show that FRAP provides consistent and substantial improvements over representative performance-estimation methods under distribution shift.

</details>


### [112] [Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06344)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出等变神经置信传播(ENBP)，一种因子图框架，在SE(3)对称性下传递高斯混合消息并合成秩2精度矩阵，在分子构象生成和机器人推理任务上实现超100倍加速和更高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变网络只能输出标量和向量，无法表达各向异性不确定性所需的秩2精度张量；单分量消息会坍塌多模态能量景观至无物理意义的平均值。

Method: 构建因子图框架，消息为等变高斯混合模型；通过等变外积合成秩2精度矩阵，经可微谱分解处理，采用贪心KL散度混合约简（可证与SE(3)交换）。

Result: GEOM-QM9和GEOM-Drugs上达到98.9%构象覆盖率、0.090Å误差、亚秒级延迟（比扩散基线快100倍以上）；多体机器人推理中，15+智能体时vanilla loopy BP发散而ENBP收敛，近零碰撞率，等变误差达机器精度(10⁻⁷ vs. 10⁻¹)。

Conclusion: ENBP通过等变高斯混合消息传递和秩2精度矩阵合成，有效解决多模态SE(3)推理问题，在分子构象和机器人推理上显著超越现有方法。

Abstract: Probabilistic inference over spatially embedded variables requires beliefs that respect $SE(3)$ symmetry, yet existing equivariant networks produce only scalars and vectors -- not the rank-2 precision tensors needed for anisotropic uncertainty, and single-component messages collapse multi-modal energy landscapes to physically meaningless averages. We introduce Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation (ENBP), a factor-graph framework whose messages are equivariant Gaussian mixture models with sufficient statistics that transform exactly under $SE(3)$. Rank-2 precision matrices are synthesised via equivariant outer products, ingested through differentiable spectral decomposition, and kept tractable by a greedy KL-based mixture reduction that provably commutes with $SE(3)$. On GEOM-QM9 and GEOM-Drugs, ENBP achieves 98.9% conformational coverage at 0.090 $\mathring{A}$ error with sub-second latency -- over $100\times$ faster than diffusion baselines at higher accuracy. On multi-body robotic inference, vanilla loopy BP diverges at 15+ agents while ENBP converges with near-zero collision rates and machine-precision equivariance error (${\sim}10^{-7}$ vs.\ $10^{-1}$ for augmented baselines).

</details>


### [113] [Performance Evaluation of GraphCast for Medium-Range Weather Forecasting over Brazil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06348)
*Wolfgang R. Rowell,Lucas S. Kupssinskü*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了GraphCast与ECMWF IFS HRES在巴西四个气候子区域的预报表现，发现GraphCast在冬季中程（2-7天）对快速传播的斜压系统（Z500）表现不佳，但在延伸期具有优势；在夏季湿季，GraphCast能准确捕捉大尺度水汽输送但会抑制高频对流变率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLWP模型在全球尺度上表现出色，但它们在以复杂强对流环境为特征的全球南方地区（特别是巴西）的效能尚未得到充分验证，缺乏区域基准评估。

Method: 采用可扩展的云原生管道和WeatherBench-X框架，选取四个巴西气候子区域、四个季节窗口，以IFS分析为真值，评估T850、Q850、Z500三个对流层变量的统计指标，对比GraphCast与ECMWF IFS HRES。

Result: 冬季：GraphCast在2-7天中程对快速传播的斜压系统（Z500）表现逊色，但在延伸期因平滑混沌小尺度变率而占优。夏季湿季：GraphCast能准确捕捉大尺度水汽输送，但会内在地抑制高频对流变率。

Conclusion: 建立了巴西区域的MLWP模型评估基线，定义了指导未来“热带化”努力的物理边界条件，以优化基础AI模型对区域韧性的适应能力。

Abstract: The paradigm of global weather forecasting is rapidly shifting with the emergence of Machine Learning Weather Prediction models (MLWP). While these data-driven architectures demonstrate remarkable global skill, regional benchmarks in the Global South remain scarce, leaving their efficacy in complex, highly convective environments largely unverified. This study evaluates the performance of GraphCast operational against the deterministic ECMWF IFS HRES as baseline across four distinct Brazilian climatic sub-regions. Utilizing a scalable, cloud-native pipeline and the WeatherBench-X framework for benchmarking weather models, we assess selected tropospheric variables ($T_{850}$, $Q_{850}$, $Z_{500}$) over four selected seasonal windows, employing the operational IFS analysis as the ground truth to calculate the statistical metrics for both models. Results reveal a regime-dependent skill profile. During the austral winter, GraphCast underperforms in the medium range (lead days 2-7) for $Z_{500}$ when resolving fast-propagating baroclinic systems over southern Brazil, but regains an advantage in the extended range, where its inherent smoothing of chaotic small-scale variability becomes beneficial under deterministic skill metrics. Conversely, during the austral summer wet season, GraphCast accurately captures large-scale moisture transport while intrinsically dampening the high-frequency convective variability that degrades deterministic NWP temperature forecasts. These findings establish a baseline for Brazil and define the specific physical boundaries that will guide future ``tropicalization'' efforts, aiming to optimize these foundational AI models for regional resilience.

</details>


### [114] [Maximising the Set-Piece Return: Optimising Football Corner Tactics with Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06353)
*Sean Groom,Michael Groom,Francisco Belo,Axl Rice,Liam Anderson,Victor-Alexandru Darvariu,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图强化学习的角球战术优化框架，通过调整进攻球员位置和速度最大化首次触球射门概率，在3000+英超角球数据上优于传统优化方法，实现从历史模仿到奖励驱动战术发现的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球战术评估方法局限于描述历史行为或分析师指定的反事实场景，缺乏发现新策略的能力，亟需超越历史模式模仿、实现可泛化的战术优化。

Method: 将角球战术优化建模为决策问题，使用图结构数据上的强化学习架构学习通用策略，该策略能调整任意初始球员配置的位置和速度以最大化首次触球射门概率。

Result: 在超过3000个英超角球数据上的评估表明，该方法在相同推理预算下显著优于基线优化技术。

Conclusion: 图强化学习能够将定位球分析从历史评估和模仿转向奖励驱动的战术发现，为足球战术创新提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly employed for the evaluation of football tactics. However, existing approaches focus on characterising historical actions or analyst-specified counterfactual scenarios. In this work, we seek to go beyond the imitation of historically observed patterns towards discovering new generalisable player configurations and strategies. To tackle this, we focus on optimising corner kick routines, and formulate a decision-making problem in which a central policy makes adjustments to attacking player positions and velocities to maximise first contact shot probability. Unlike classic optimisation that solves for isolated setups, we contribute a reinforcement learning architecture operating on graph-structured data that yields a general policy for adjusting arbitrary starting player positions. Evaluated on over 3,000 Premier League corners, our approach strongly outperforms baseline optimisation techniques under matched inference budgets. Our results suggest that graph reinforcement learning can shift set-piece analysis from historical evaluation and imitation towards reward-driven tactical discovery.

</details>


### [115] [Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator for HEC-RAS 2D Water-Surface Elevation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06385)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Mahdi Abdelguerfi,Julian Simeonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LRFIO，一种基于本征单元格的替代模型，用于HEC-RAS 2D中与求解器一致的水面高程预测，无需栅格重映射误差。该模型通过增量预测和惯性响应算子，在不同基准上实现自适应复杂度控制，最大验证遗憾4.30%，部署时间0.003-0.242秒，在Beaver Bayou案例中获得2.75×10^4倍于HEC-RAS的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有替代模型通常依赖栅格化方法，引入重映射误差和信息混淆。本文旨在开发一种直接作用于原始非均匀计算网格的替代模型，避免上述问题，并在保持求解器一致性的前提下实现高效预测。

Method: 提出LRFIO（学习型响应场惯性算子），一种无外力项的增量式替代模型。从HEC-RAS求解轨迹中校准惯性响应算子，通过封闭形式的本征单元滚动部署。采用基例优先响应层次（持续性→全局校准惯性→分段响应场惯性），将分段、残差校正和神经化惯性作为可学习建模选择，仅在有验证证据支持时保留增加复杂度。

Result: 在4个不同HEC-RAS 2D基准上评估，LRFIO为不同域保留不同响应结构，实现自适应复杂度；最大验证遗憾4.30%；部署滚动时间0.003-0.242秒；Beaver Bayou实测-求解对比获得2.75×10^4倍（归一化时域）加速比。

Conclusion: 本征单元格增量是强求解器条件预测支架；增加响应场、神经或空间复杂度应仅在经验证证明合理时保留。

Abstract: This article presents a cross-dataset evaluation of learned native-cell surrogate models for solver-consistent water-surface elevation (WSE) prediction in HEC-RAS 2D. To avoid raster remapping error and information-access confounding, surrogates are evaluated directly on the original nonuniform computational cells under an explicit policy that separates static project inputs, current hydraulic state, project-input forcing, calibration-derived quantities, and future solver-output targets. We introduce the Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator (LRFIO), a no-forcing, increment-based learned surrogate that calibrates an inertial response operator from solved HEC-RAS trajectories and deploys the retained operator through closed-form native-cell rollout. LRFIO evaluates a base-case-first response hierarchy consisting of persistence, global calibrated inertia, and segmented response-field inertia. Segmentation, residual correction, and neuralized inertia are treated as learnable modeling choices, with added complexity retained only when validation evidence justifies its cost. Evaluated across four diverse HEC-RAS 2D benchmarks, LRFIO retains different response structures for different domains, demonstrating adaptive learned complexity. The selector audit shows controlled complexity with a maximum validation regret of 4.30%. During deployment, retained rollout times range from 0.003 s to 0.242 s, and the Beaver Bayou measured-solve comparison gives an estimated 2.75 x 10^4 horizon-normalized speedup over HEC-RAS. These results indicate that the current native-cell increment is a strong solver-conditioned predictive scaffold and that added response-field, neural, or spatial complexity should be retained only when empirically justified.

</details>


### [116] [Proper Scoring Rules for Right-Censored Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06393)
*Jef Jonkers,Glenn Van Wallendael,Luc Duchateau,Sofie Van Hoecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对右删失生存数据，提出一个基于“先通过删失机制映射预测分布，再应用原始恰当评分规则”的通用框架，推导出固定删失时间的局部化评分和随机删失时的边际化评分，恢复似然和IPCW准则，并得到右删失版本的CRPS、pinball损失、Brier评分和能量评分。边际化评分在条件独立删失下恰当且在可识别区域严格恰当，并引出删失engression。实验表明，该方法正确排序oracle预测，优于预测依赖的加权评分；删失engression也显著优于朴素训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恰当的评分规则为概率预测的训练与评估提供了严格理论基础，但右删失下事件时间仅被部分观测，标准形式的评分规则无法直接适用。现有方法在处理删失时存在排名反转等问题，亟需一个理论严谨且统一的框架。

Method: 提出一个通用框架：先将预测分布通过删失机制映射到观测数据上的分布，再对观测数据分布应用底层恰当评分规则。固定删失时间时得到局部化评分；随机删失或部分观测时构造边际化评分。该框架恢复似然和IPCW准则，并推导出CRPS、pinball损失、Brier评分和能量评分的右删失版本。进一步提出删失engression作为样本学习目标。

Result: 理论上证明边际化评分在条件独立删失下恰当、在可识别区域严格恰当。实验中，该框架正确排序oracle预测，而预测依赖的加权评分可能出现排名反转。删失engression显著优于直接对删失结果进行朴素训练。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为右删失生存数据的恰当评分提供了系统的理论基础，统一并扩展了现有方法，同时引入删失engression这一新工具，在理论和实验中均表现出色。

Abstract: Proper scoring rules provide a rigorous theoretical basis for the training and evaluation of probabilistic forecasts. However, in the presence of right censoring, the event time is only partially observed, rendering conventional scoring rules inapplicable in their standard form. We propose a framework for proper scoring of right-censored survival outcomes based on a simple idea: first, map the predictive distribution through the censoring mechanism, then apply the underlying proper score on the induced observed-data law. This yields localized scores for fixed censoring times and marginalized scores when the censoring time is random or only partially observed. The resulting construction recovers familiar right-censored likelihood and IPCW-type criteria within a coherent framework, while also yielding right-censored versions of the CRPS, pinball loss, Brier score, and energy score. We show that the marginalized score is proper under conditional independent censoring and strictly proper on the identifiable region. The same principle also leads to censored engression, a sample-based learning objective for multivariate right-censored survival modeling. In experiments, our scores correctly rank the oracle forecast across several censoring regimes, whereas forecast-dependent plug-in weighted scores can exhibit ranking reversals. Censored engression likewise substantially improves over naive training on censored outcomes.

</details>


### [117] [The Post-GCN Decade Revisited: Curvature-Stratified Evaluation of Relational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06397)
*Shuo Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Quanxin Wang,Bailin Wu,Bokui Wang,Shunyang Huang,Boyan Deng,Haonan Liu,Ruiyi Fang,Zhenxiang Xu,Boyu Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当前关系学习评估中依赖扁平排行榜存在的系统性偏差，提出了一种基于曲率分层的评估框架。实验表明模型排名在相同曲率区间内高度稳定，但跨区间显著变化，证明模型性能本质上依赖于几何特性而非普遍可迁移的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关系学习评估依赖扁平排行榜，平均跨异构数据集的性能，隐含假设了统一的底层结构。这种假设会引入系统性偏差：掩盖依赖于几何的性能变化，导致关于模型泛化的误导性结论。

Method: 提出曲率分层评估框架，将数据集按曲率划分为正曲率、负曲率和近零曲率三个区域。在14个数据集上评估了18个代表性模型（包括GCN、图基座模型和表格学习方法）。

Result: 模型排名在同一曲率区间内高度稳定，但跨区间显著变化，表明性能根本上依赖于几何特性。发现GFM在与几何对齐的GNN相比时存在收益递减的区间。

Conclusion: 提出了一种几何感知的评估协议，能产生比标准聚合基准更可靠和可解释的比较。释放了所有代码、曲率分层数据集划分和评估工具。

Abstract: Current evaluation practices in relational learning rely heavily on flat leaderboards that average performance across heterogeneous datasets, implicitly assuming a uniform underlying structure. We show that this assumption introduces systematic bias: it obscures geometry-dependent performance variations and can lead to misleading conclusions about model generalization. In this work, we identify intrinsic geometry as a key latent factor governing model effectiveness. We demonstrate that conventional aggregated metrics mask critical performance trade-offs that only become visible when datasets are stratified by their geometric properties. To address this issue, we introduce a curvature-stratified evaluation framework that partitions datasets into positive, negative, and near-zero curvature regimes. Our benchmark evaluates 18 representative models including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), and tabular learning methods across 14 datasets. We find that model rankings are highly stable within each curvature regime but shift significantly across regimes, indicating that performance is fundamentally geometry-dependent rather than universally transferable. Notably, we identify regimes where GFMs offer diminishing returns compared to geometry-aligned GNNs. Based on these findings, we propose a geometry-aware evaluation protocol that yields more reliable and interpretable comparisons than standard aggregated benchmarks. We release all code, curvature-stratified dataset splits, and evaluation tools to support reproducible and rigorous assessment of future relational learning methods. Code and datasets are provided in our project homepage: https://sirbabbage.github.io/CurvBench_HOME/.

</details>


### [118] [Double Preconditioning (DoPr): Optimization for Test-Time Performance, not Validation Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06418)
*Thomas T. Zhang,Alok Shah,Yifei Zhang,Vincent Zhang,Nikolai Matni,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双预条件（DoPr）优化范式，通过结合梯度预条件（如Adam/Muon）和激活预条件（如KFAC），专门解决深度学习中测试时反馈（TTF）导致的误差累积问题，在多种TTF场景下提升下游性能，且这种提升并不总是伴随验证损失的改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中许多模型使用单步预测损失训练，但在部署时需沿自身预测进行 rollout（如自回归语言模型、流生成模型、机器人策略学习），这导致训练/验证损失与下游指标（任务成功率、生成质量）之间存在不匹配，且差距随任务长度增长而扩大。现有工作主要从数据、架构、目标设计角度缓解该问题，本文提出从优化角度入手。

Method: 提出双预条件（DoPr）优化范式，将梯度预条件（Adam/Muon等）与激活预条件（AP，如KFAC）相结合，利用激活预条件处理误差累积问题，同时保留梯度预条件的优势。

Result: 在多种TTF设置下，DoPr能有效提升下游模型性能，且这种提升不总是与验证损失的改善同步发生。

Conclusion: DoPr作为一种即插即用的优化干预手段，能有效缓解测试时反馈问题；同时，测试时性能提升与验证损失改善的不一致性，引发了对单步监督目标下模型评估方式的重新思考。

Abstract: Many modern applications of deep learning involve training a neural network via a one-step prediction loss (e.g., $L^2$ regression, cross-entropy), but deploy the network by rolling out along its own predictions. Key examples include autoregressive language modeling, flow-based generative modeling, and robot policy learning. It is well-documented that these settings induce a phenomenon we call test-time feedback (TTF): the mismatch between the training/validation loss and downstream metrics of interest, such as task success rate and generation quality, which grows with task length. While data curation, architecture, and objective design have been proposed to combat train-test shift in TTF settings, this paper proposes optimization as a new design axis to mitigate error accumulation. Specifically, we introduce a new optimization paradigm called double-preconditioning (DoPr) uniquely tailored to the challenges of TTF. DoPr combines gradient-wise preconditioning, as in Adam and Muon, with activation-wise preconditioning (AP), such as in KFAC. We show that the addition of AP yields a drop-in intervention for increasing downstream model performance across a range of TTF settings. Interestingly, these gains in test-time performance do not consistently accompany improvements in validation loss, opening new questions about how to properly evaluate models trained with one-step supervised objectives.

</details>


### [119] [In-Context Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06458)
*Alexander Möllers,Marvin Sextro,Julius Hense,Gabriel Dernbach,Klaus-Robert Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Perceiver架构的上下文学习器，通过在合成数据上预训练，能够在少量标注包的情况下解决多实例学习（MIL）任务，无需梯度更新即可在单次前向传播中完成分类。混合使用多种合成数据生成器进行预训练，模型在12个MIL基准测试中平均表现最佳，超越了需要特定任务训练的监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIL算法在低标签数据场景下表现不佳：灵活模型容易过拟合，而刚性模型难以适应任务需求。因此需要一种能够从少量标注包中快速适应新任务的方法。

Method: 采用Perceiver风格的架构构建上下文学习器，在合成数据上进行预训练。提出了多种合成数据生成器用于生成包结构数据，并研究了它们各自捕捉的归纳偏置。最终模型混合使用这些生成器进行预训练，推理时通过单次前向传播完成分类，无需梯度更新。

Result: 在12个MIL基准测试上，预训练于混合合成数据生成器的模型取得了最佳平均性能，超越了需要任务特定训练的有监督基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过合成数据预训练的上下文学习器能够有效解决低标签场景下的MIL问题，不同合成数据生成器提供互补的归纳偏置，混合使用可提升模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) addresses problems where supervision is available at the level of bags of instances and has been successfully applied in fields ranging from computational pathology to satellite imagery. Nevertheless, existing algorithms struggle in the low-label regime that characterizes many real-world applications. Flexible models overfit and rigid ones fail to adapt to the task at hand. We show that pretraining an in-context learner with a Perceiver-style architecture on synthetic data yields a model that can solve new tasks from a handful of labeled bags. At inference time, classification happens in a single forward pass and requires no gradient updates. We propose and investigate different synthetic data generators for bag-structured data and find that they capture complementary inductive biases. A model pretrained on a mixture of these generators inherits their per-task strengths and achieves the best average performance across twelve MIL benchmarks, outperforming supervised baselines that require task-specific training.

</details>


### [120] [Event Detection for Parameter-to-KPI Dependency Learning for AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06459)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对AI-RAN/O-RAN架构中多个AI驱动的网络控制功能相互干扰的问题，提出了将连续遥测数据转换为二元事件指标（参数活动与KPI响应）的显著性检测方法，并利用合成闭环流量生成器进行验证。实验表明，当信号与背景噪声充分分离时，该方法能可靠恢复潜在依赖结构，阈值校准是影响事件检测质量的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络中多个AI驱动的控制功能同时运行时会产生相互干扰，而仅靠原始网络数据难以检测这种交互。当前缺少一种可靠、可解释的依赖结构来捕捉控制参数与网络性能结果之间的实时影响关系。

Method: 将连续遥测数据转换为二元事件指标（参数活动与KPI响应）的问题建模为显著性检测问题；构建合成闭环流量生成器（包含植入的潜在依赖关系），用于评估基于机器学习的依赖恢复流水线。

Result: 当信号与背景噪声充分分离时，所提流水线能够可靠地从噪声连续轨迹中恢复潜在依赖结构；阈值校准是控制事件检测质量的关键因素。

Conclusion: 该工作为自适应AI-RAN控制系统中可解释的依赖学习奠定了基础，下一步方向是完善依赖结构学习以支持多控制功能协同优化。

Abstract: Next-generation wireless networks are expected to rely on multiple concurrent AI-driven control functions that optimize different network objectives simultaneously, particularly in AI-integrated and open radio access network architectures such as AI Radio Access Network (AI-RAN) and Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN). When these functions interact, they can interfere with one another in ways that are difficult to detect from raw network data alone. A key missing piece for managing such interactions is a reliable, interpretable dependency structure that captures which control parameters are actively influencing which network performance outcomes at any given time. This paper focuses on the event-detection step needed to support such dependency learning by converting noisy continuous telemetry into binary indicators of parameter activity and KPI response. The central difficulty is that not every fluctuation in the data reflects a genuine control interaction, so the method must distinguish real parameter-outcome relationships from background variation. Because real AI-RAN traffic traces with known parameter-KPI ground truth are difficult to obtain, we introduce a synthetic closed-loop traffic generator with planted latent dependencies. We use this controlled telemetry to evaluate a machine-learning-based dependency recovery pipeline that formulates the conversion of continuous traces into binary event indicators as a significance-detection problem. Experimental evaluation shows that the proposed pipeline reliably recovers the latent dependency structure from noisy continuous traces when the signal is sufficiently separated from background variation, while highlighting threshold calibration as the key factor controlling event-detection quality. These results constitute a foundational step toward interpretable dependency learning for adaptive AI-RAN control systems.

</details>


### [121] [PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06470)
*Senmiao Wang,Tiantian Fang,Haoran Zhang,Yushun Zhang,Kunxiang Zhao,Alex Schwing,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种预条件（PC）层，通过多项式预条件子对权重矩阵进行参数化，在LLM训练过程中保持权重的稳定条件数。PC模块通过低阶多项式预条件重塑权重矩阵的奇异值谱。训练后可合并回原始架构，不引入推理开销。在Llama-1B预训练中，PC层相比标准Transformer在AdamW和Muon优化器下均表现更优。理论上，通过证明均匀约束每层奇异值能保证深层线性网络的梯度下降几何收敛到全局最小值，论证了谱控制原理的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型（LLM）训练过程中权重矩阵条件数不稳定导致优化困难的问题，通过谱控制提升训练稳定性与收敛效率。

Method: 提出预条件（PC）层，作为权重参数化方法，利用低阶多项式预条件子调整权重矩阵的奇异值分布，在训练后可将预条件权重合并回原始架构，无推理额外开销。

Result: 在Llama-1B规模预训练任务中，PC层相较于标准Transformer在AdamW和Muon两种优化器下均取得更优表现。

Conclusion: PC层作为一种轻量级、无推理开销的模块，能有效改善LLM训练的权重条件数，且理论上证明了谱控制对梯度收敛到全局最小值的保障作用。

Abstract: We propose a preconditioning (PC) layer, a weight parameterization via polynomial preconditioner that ensures stable weight conditioning throughout LLM training. The PC module reshapes the singular-value spectrum of weight matrices via low-degree polynomial preconditioning. After training, the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture, incurring no inference overhead. We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-training, for both the AdamW and Muon optimizers. Theoretically, we justify this spectrum-control principle by proving that uniformly bounding each layer's singular values ensures geometric convergence of gradient descent to global minima, for certain deep linear networks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer.

</details>


### [122] [RREDCoT: Segment-Level Reward Redistribution for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06475)
*Mykyta Ielanskyi,Kajetan Schweighofer,Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RREDCoT方法，通过模型自身进行奖励再分配来解决推理语言模型中GRPO算法因延迟奖励导致的高方差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等强化学习方法在推理语言模型中存在延迟奖励导致的高方差问题，蒙特卡洛采样虽能提供无偏估计但计算开销过大，不适用于长时间序列的信用分配。

Method: 提出RREDCoT，利用模型自身近似最优奖励再分配，无需额外生成；涉及思维链轨迹的分段和状态值估计等关键设计。

Result: 与蒙特卡洛采样及多种归因方法相比，RREDCoT在性能上具有优势。

Conclusion: 基于模型自身的奖励再分配是解决推理语言模型中延迟奖励问题的有效途径，在计算效率与信用分配精度之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Recent advancements in reasoning language models have been driven by Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning. Most often, these rely on the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) algorithm or modifications thereof to steer the models to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces. The final answer can only be verified, and the reward assigned, after the CoT trace is complete, making it a delayed reward problem. GRPO and its modifications correspond to Monte Carlo methods in standard RL, which are known to suffer from high variance. A possible solution to this problem is the redistribution of rewards through credit assignment, where segments of the CoT trace that are important for arriving at the desirable solution are emphasized by assigning a higher reward. While Monte Carlo sampling can be used to provide an unbiased estimate of intermediate state values, its computational overhead makes it unsuitable for train-time credit assignment in long contexts at high granularity. We introduce RREDCoT (Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts), which utilizes the model itself to approximate the optimal reward redistribution without additional generation. We investigate the advantages of our method compared to MC sampling and several attribution methods. We further analyze several aspects relevant to the construction of the redistribution such as segmentation of CoT traces and state value estimation.

</details>


### [123] [Pretraining Recurrent Networks without Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06479)
*Akarsh Kumar,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出监督记忆训练（SMT），通过将RNN训练简化为一步记忆转换标签上的监督学习，实现时间并行训练和稳定的O(1)梯度路径，在语言建模等任务上优于BPTT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BPTT在训练RNN时存在时序上的串行限制（限制并行性）以及梯度消失/爆炸问题（难以学习长程依赖），需要一种能并行训练且稳定传播梯度的替代方法。

Method: SMT包含两步：(1) 使用基于Transformer的编码器在预测状态目标上训练，生成记忆转换标签(m_t, x_{t+1}) → m_{t+1}；(2) 将RNN训练转化为对这些一步记忆标签的监督学习，无需展开RNN即可实现时间并行训练，梯度路径长度恒为O(1)。

Result: 在语言建模和像素序列建模等任务上，SMT在预训练多种RNN架构时均优于BPTT，能更好地捕捉长程依赖并实现并行训练。

Conclusion: SMT通过解耦"记住什么"与"如何更新记忆"，为非线性RNN提供了一种可并行、梯度稳定的训练范式，有望推动基于时序抽象建模的模型扩展。

Abstract: Training recurrent neural networks (RNNs) requires assigning credit across long sequences of computations. Standard backpropagation through time (BPTT) addresses this problem poorly: it is sequential in time, limiting parallelism, and suffers from vanishing or exploding gradients, making long-range associations difficult to learn. We propose Supervised Memory Training (SMT), a method for training nonlinear RNNs that sidesteps recurrent credit propagation entirely by reducing RNN training to supervised learning on one-step memory transition labels $(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$. SMT acquires these memory labels by training a Transformer-based encoder on a predictive state objective--retaining only information from the past necessary to predict the future. By decoupling what to remember from how to update memory, SMT enables time-parallel RNN training with a stable $O(1)$ length gradient path between any two tokens--without ever unrolling the RNN. We find that SMT outperforms BPTT when pretraining various RNN architectures on tasks like language modeling and pixel sequence modeling. SMT enables nonlinear RNNs to better capture long-range dependencies and train in parallel, potentially unlocking the scaling of models that build temporal abstractions of past experience.

</details>


### [124] [Regret Minimization with Adaptive Opponents in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06486)
*Mingyang Liu,Asuman Ozdaglar,Tiancheng Yu,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的博弈论遗憾度量——重复策略遗憾（RP-Regret），用于衡量在具有自适应对手的重复博弈中的学习性能。该度量考虑了玩家基于历史进行反事实推理的能力，比现有遗憾概念更具优势。文章证明了在特定条件下可实现亚线性RP-Regret，并提出了三种优化算法。当所有玩家都最小化该遗憾时，可以学习到某些子博弈完美均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的外部遗憾度量无法捕捉自适应对手的应对行为，需要一种能够反映玩家反事实推理能力的博弈论遗憾度量，以更好地分析重复博弈中的学习与均衡达成问题。

Method: 定义了重复策略遗憾（RP-Regret），分析了实现亚线性RP-Regret的必要条件（关于比较器策略的变化率及记忆长度），并提出了三种算法：(i) 基于优化oracle的方法；(ii) 最小化凸线性化代理遗憾的方法；(iii) 在对手策略缓慢变化时直接最小化RP-Regret的方法。

Result: 证明了在特定条件下RP-Regret可以达到亚线性增长；当所有玩家都最小化RP-Regret或其线性化变体时，可以学习到重复博弈的某些子博弈完美均衡；实验表明最小化该遗憾能在Stag-Hunt博弈中实现更合作的、更高效用的解决方案。

Conclusion: RP-Regret是重复博弈中一个有效的性能度量，克服了传统外部遗憾在自适应对手环境下的局限，为学习合作均衡提供了理论基础和可行算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we study regret minimization in repeated games with \emph{adaptive} opponents who can respond based on histories of play. The standard metric of \emph{external regret} in online learning is known to fail to capture such adaptivity. To account for players' counterfactual reasoning, we introduce {\tt Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)}, a game-theoretic metric that measures the difference between the \emph{realized} and the \emph{best-in-hindsight} accumulated utility when all players can \emph{respond} to the history of play. Compared to existing regret notions in this setting, ours is native to repeated game playing, enabling stronger comparators and opponents with fewer constraints, while maintaining the possibility of finding better equilibria when all players minimize it. We first identify necessary conditions for obtaining {\tt RP-Regret} sublinear in time, on the variation of the player's comparator strategies in the regret definition and on the memories of both the comparator and opponents' strategies. We then study additional conditions and provable algorithms to minimize {\tt RP-Regret}, which is by definition \emph{non-convex} in the strategy space. To address this challenge, we propose three algorithms: (i) one based on an optimization oracle, as assumed in some prior work in online non-convex learning; (ii) one that minimizes a convex and \emph{linearized} surrogate of {\tt RP-Regret} at each iteration; (iii) one that directly minimizes {\tt RP-Regret} when opponents change strategies slowly. Furthermore, when all players can run algorithms to minimize the {\tt RP-Regret} (or its linearized variant), certain subgame perfect equilibria of the repeated game can be learned. We also provide experiments showing that minimizing our regret notions can lead to more cooperative solutions with higher utility in games such as Stag-Hunt.

</details>


### [125] [TailLoR: Protecting Principal Components in Parameter-Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06494)
*Marius Dragoi,Ioana Pintilie,Alexandra Dragomir,Antonio Barbalau,Florin Brad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailLoR是一种基于谱分解的参数高效持续学习方法，通过固定预训练权重的奇异基U和V作为参考框架，对奇异值矩阵进行低秩更新，并引入软谱惩罚机制抑制对主导奇异方向的更新，从而将细粒度适应引导至灵活的长尾谱坐标，减少干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于谱分解的参数高效微调方法在持续学习中取得了进展，但在如何减少新旧任务之间的干扰、同时保持模型对细粒度特征的适应能力方面仍存在挑战。

Method: TailLoR利用预训练权重的奇异基U和V作为固定参考框架，仅对奇异值矩阵进行低秩更新。引入软谱惩罚机制，抑制对主导奇异方向（对应较大奇异值）的更新，从而将模型适应能力引导至高度灵活的长尾谱坐标（对应较小奇异值）。

Result: 该方法能够有效减少持续学习中不同任务之间的干扰，同时保持对细粒度特征的适应能力。

Conclusion: TailLoR通过谱空间中的定向低秩更新和软惩罚机制，为持续学习提供了一种高效且干扰可控的参数微调方法。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning methods based on spectral decomposition have enabled progress in Continual Learning. In this paper we introduce TailLoR, which utilizes the singular bases U and V of the pre-trained weights as a fixed reference frame to learn a low-rank update applied to the singular value matrix. A soft spectral penalty discourages updates aligned with dominant singular directions, reducing interference while routing fine-grained adaptation into the highly flexible, long-tail spectral coordinates.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
*Kokil Jaidka,Saifuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了Reddit r/ChangeMyView中被中止的AI干预实验数据，发现AI生成评论在身份表演、权威信号、结盟策略和认知偏见触发方面呈现系统性特征，与人类评论存在显著差异，表明仅靠披露机制无法解决AI在辩论环境中造成的认知不对称问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型在未被披露的情况下如何在身份丰富的审议论坛中运作，以及AI生成内容与人类内容在说服策略上的差异。

Method: 对Reddit r/ChangeMyView平台被中止的AI干预实验公开数据集进行结构化内容分析，评估AI评论的身份表演、权威信号、结盟策略和认知启发式激活模式，并与人类创作的CMV反论点进行对比。

Result: 超过三分之二的AI评论涉及身份定位或采用；几乎所有评论包含结盟动作和权威声明；大多数评论触发认知偏见触发器（尤其是确认偏见、代表性启发和可得性启发）。这些模式系统共现，形成以说服效率为导向的修辞架构。与人类评论相比，AI在每个维度上呈现反转分布：权威使用更密集、结盟更具对抗性、更依赖外部引用而非经验基础。

Conclusion: 在AI参与的辩论环境中，真实与合成的认知立场之间的区分日益模糊，仅靠披露要求无法解决这一问题。研究指向需要建立能够评估AI系统如何构建可信度（而不仅仅是检测其存在）的审计框架。

Abstract: This study analyzes a publicly released dataset from a discontinued field experiment on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView. The intervention, conducted by unknown, external researchers and halted following ethical backlash, involved undisclosed AI-generated accounts engaging users in live debate. After public disclosure, Reddit authorized moderators to release an archive of the AI-generated comments, creating a rare opportunity to examine how large language models operated in an identity-rich deliberative forum without disclosure. We conduct a structured content analysis of this corpus, evaluating identity performance, authority signaling, alignment strategies, and activation of cognitive heuristics. Identity targeting or adoption appears in over two-thirds of comments, alignment moves and authority claims in nearly all of them, and cognitive-bias triggers -- particularly confirmation bias, representativeness, and availability -- in the large majority. These patterns co-occur systematically, composing a rhetorical architecture calibrated for persuasive efficiency rather than authentic deliberative participation. Compared against human-authored CMV counter-arguments, the agents inverted the typical distribution on every dimension: denser authority use, more adversarial alignment, and heavier reliance on external citation over experiential grounding. In such environments, distinctions between authentic and synthetic epistemic standing grow increasingly opaque -- an asymmetry that disclosure mandates alone cannot address. The results point toward auditing frameworks capable of assessing how AI systems structure credibility, not merely whether they are present.

</details>


### [127] [What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
*Chen Huang,Yuhao Wu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACT方法，将多智能体系统中的自由文本通信转化为紧凑的动作-状态记录，从而在保持任务性能的同时显著降低token消耗。实验表明，PACT在不同MAS拓扑结构下均能改善性能-成本权衡，在OpenHands和SWE-agent等生产环境中也能实现token大幅减少而性能不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中，智能体之间采用自由形式的自然语言通信，导致token使用量快速增长、共享上下文窗口被过度消耗，进而影响系统性能和推理成本。亟需一种更高效的智能体间通信策略。

Method: PACT（Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission）将智能体间通信视为公开状态更新问题，在每条原始智能体输出进入共享历史之前，将其投影为紧凑的动作-状态记录。该方法基于"有效通信始终保留下游智能体所需的以动作为中心的信息"这一发现。

Result: 在不同MAS拓扑下，PACT一致改善了性能-成本权衡，用更少的token实现相当或更强的任务性能。在OpenHands上，PACT以-10% token-per-resolved提升了resolve率；在SWE-agent上保持resolve中性而输入token减半。

Conclusion: 没有固定的单一通信策略在所有场景下普遍最优，而PACT作为一种协议化的动作-状态通信方法，能够有效压缩智能体间通信，在多种MAS拓扑和生产级编码任务中实现更优的性能-成本权衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models are typically organized around roles, pipelines, and turn schedules, while the content that agents pass to one another is often left as unconstrained natural language. However, this free-form communication can rapidly inflate token usage, consume the shared context window, and ultimately affect both system performance and inference cost. We analyze five common inter-agent communication strategies across two MAS topologies, finding that no fixed strategy is universally optimal. Instead, effective inter-agent messages consistently preserve action-centered information needed by downstream agents. Building on this, we propose the PACT (Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission), which treats inter-agent communication as a public state-update problem and projects each raw agent output into a compact action-state record before it enters shared history. Across different MAS topologies, PACT consistently improves the performance-cost trade-off, achieving comparable or stronger task performance with substantially fewer tokens. The gains extend to production coding harnesses: PACT lifts OpenHands' resolve rate at -10% tokens-per-resolved, and is resolve-neutral on SWE-agent while halving input tokens. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT.

</details>


### [128] [I Know What You Meme, Even If it Emerged Today: Understanding Evolving Memes through Open-World Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05316)
*Shanhong Liu,Rui Cao,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种零样本框架Query Retrieve Conclude，通过识别缺失知识、检索开放网络证据、综合背景知识来理解和检测多模态表情包，在多个数据集上优于零样本基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽视了多模态表情包理解所需的动态背景知识，或仅依赖预训练模型的固定参数知识，导致对新兴表情包的知识不完整、过时或不可用。

Method: 提出Query Retrieve Conclude零样本框架，包含三个步骤：识别缺失知识（Query）、检索开放网络证据（Retrieve）、综合证据生成背景知识（Conclude），用于表情包理解和检测。

Result: 在三个表情包理解数据集和五个表情包检测任务上，该框架在知识恢复、表情包理解和下游检测方面均优于零样本基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过动态检索外部知识而非依赖固定参数知识，可以有效提升新兴表情包的理解和检测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal memes are dynamic and often require up to date background knowledge for interpretation. Existing methods often overlook such knowledge or rely on fixed parametric knowledge of pretrained models that may be incomplete, outdated, or unavailable for emerging memes. We introduce Query Retrieve Conclude, a zero shot framework that identifies missing knowledge, retrieves open web evidence, and synthesizes evidence grounded background knowledge for meme understanding and detection. We also introduce a curated meme understanding benchmark of recent memes from 2024 to 2026 with external background knowledge annotations. Experiments on three meme understanding datasets and five meme detection tasks show that our framework improves knowledge recovery, meme understanding and downstream detection over zero shot baselines.

</details>


### [129] [GITCO: Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization in TSFMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05332)
*Manya Pandey,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对基于补丁的时间序列基础模型在零样本预测中因结构异常补丁导致的上下文中毒问题，提出GITCO框架，通过在推理时优化输入上下文（而非修改模型权重）来提升预测精度，在TimesFM 2.5上平均降低1.95%的MASE，并引入上下文敏感度剖面作为TSFM的新表征属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于补丁的时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）存在上下文中毒问题：结构异常的补丁会不成比例地吸引模型注意力，悄悄降低零样本预测质量。现有方法需要修改模型权重，而本文希望在推理时通过优化输入上下文来提升精度，无需修改模型参数。

Method: 提出GITCO（门控推理时上下文优化）框架，包含三个轻量级组件：Gate（门控）、Router（路由）和Critic（评判器），通过选择性地识别并抑制有害补丁来优化输入上下文，无需任何参数更新。

Result: 在TimesFM 2.5上跨53个GIFT-Eval数据集进行K折交叉验证，GITCO平均降低1.95%的MASE，达到改进理论上限的89.9%。

Conclusion: 提出上下文敏感度剖面作为TSFM的新表征属性，描述时间序列元特征与推理时上下文干预下预期精度改进之间的映射关系，该映射由模型架构和数据统计结构共同塑造。

Abstract: Patch-based Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) suffer from context poisoning: structurally anomalous patches capture disproportionate attention and silently degrade zero-shot forecast quality. We propose improving TSFM accuracy at inference time by optimizing the input context rather than modifying model weights. We present GITCO (Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization), a lightweight three-component framework: Gate, Router, and Critic that selectively identifies and suppresses harmful patches without any parameter updates. Evaluated on TimesFM 2.5 across 53 GIFT-Eval datasets under K-fold cross-validation, GITCO achieves an average +1.95% MASE reduction on TimesFM 2.5 while capturing 89.9% of the improvement upper bound. We introduce context sensitivity profiles as a new characterizable property of TSFMs: the mapping from time series meta-features to expected accuracy improvement under inference-time context intervention, shaped jointly by model architecture and the statistical structure of the data.

</details>


### [130] [Uncertainty Aware Functional Behavior Prediction and Material Fatigue Assessment for Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05334)
*Nehal Afifi,Mehdi Khabou,Victor Mas,Jonas Hemmerich,Patric Grauberger,Stefan Dietrich,Volker Schulze,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对圆形工厂中返回产品异质退化状态下的复用决策问题，提出了一种将不确定性感知的功能预测与组件级疲劳评估相结合的实例特定可靠性工作流。该方法利用卷积编码器提取负载模式，LSTM预测九个功能变量的高斯分布，同时通过有限元应力重建和损伤评估进行疲劳分析，最终通过流式回放算法融合为可靠性轨迹。实验表明该方法在角磨机上取得了高精度预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PHM方法通常针对固定运行条件或孤立组件基准，材料疲劳评估很少与系统级功能预后关联，无法解决返回产品异质退化状态下复用决策的难题。

Method: 提出结合不确定性感知功能预测与组件级疲劳评估的实例特定可靠性工作流。卷积分编码器提取负载模式，LSTM预测九个功能变量的高斯均值和方差；同时通过有限元应力重建、S-N/Miner损伤评估（含Haibach扩展）和Paris律裂纹扩展分析进行疲劳评估；流式回放算法融合两分支。

Result: 在角磨机上，九个输出变量的平均2%容差准确率为0.9652。热变量预测接近完美，驱动电机电流和负载速度R²分别为0.9750和0.9924。扭矩历史对预测至关重要，传统LSTM在短历史设置中优于GRU和xLSTM。

Conclusion: 该方法有效融合了负载模式驱动的功能预测和基于物理的疲劳评估，为圆形工厂中异质退化产品的复用决策提供了更全面的可靠性评估框架。

Abstract: Returned products in circular factories re-enter production with heterogeneous degradation states, usage histories, and remaining capability. Reuse cannot be decided from the current inspection alone, because future function fulfillment and component integrity may evolve differently under the next service scenario. Existing PHM approaches support degradation prediction, but often target fixed operating conditions or isolated component benchmarks, while material-fatigue assessment is rarely linked to system-level functional prognosis. This paper addresses this gap for an angle grinder by combining uncertainty-aware functional prediction with component-level fatigue assessment in an instance-specific reliability workflow. The proposed framework combines the current tool state with recent force--torque usage windows. A convolutional encoder extracts loading patterns from spindle forces and shaft torque, and an LSTM backbone predicts nine functional variables as Gaussian mean and variance estimates. In parallel, the same loading history is translated into output-shaft fatigue information through finite-element-supported stress reconstruction, S--N/Miner damage evaluation with Haibach extension, and Paris-law crack-growth analysis. A streaming replay algorithm consolidates both branches into functional, material, and system reliability trajectories. Held-out tests show mean \(2\%\)-tolerance accuracy of 0.9652 across nine outputs. Thermal variables are predicted near-perfectly, while drive motor current and load speed remain the most demanding dynamic outputs, with \(R^2\) values of 0.9750 and 0.9924. Torque history is especially important for these variables, and the conventional LSTM outperforms GRU and xLSTM in the short-history setting. Reliability calibration is most informative for drive motor current, where predicted and observed exceedance probabilities ...

</details>


### [131] [SentinelBench: A Benchmark for Long-Running Monitoring Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05342)
*Matheus Kunzler Maldaner,Adam Fourney,Amanda Swearngin,Hussein Mozzanar,Gagan Bansal,Maya Murad,Rafah Hosn,Saleema Amershi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SentinelBench，一个用于评估AI代理在长时间监控任务中表现的开源基准测试，包含100个任务和10个合成网络环境，衡量任务完成度、反应时间和资源使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理默认采用连续行动模式（持续调用工具、刷新页面等），这对长时间运行的任务效率低下。更好的策略是持续关注环境，等待外部事件触发后再响应，从而在不过度消耗资源的情况下及时行动。

Method: 构建SentinelBench基准测试，包含100个任务和10个合成网络环境（邮件、日历、金融、专业社交、娱乐等）。每个环境提供实时网页界面并回放脚本化事件序列，要求代理在页面状态动态变化中进行导航和推理。评估指标包括任务完成度、反应时间和资源使用。

Result: 在三个模型和两个浏览器代理框架上建立了性能基线，证明了代理设计选择对关键指标有显著影响，且SentinelBench能够有效区分代理行为之间的有意义差异。

Conclusion: 长时间运行的监控任务更适合采用"持续关注"策略而非"连续行动"策略，SentinelBench可以衡量响应性与成本之间的权衡，为未来研究提供了评估工具和基线。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly asked to carry out work that spans minutes, hours, or longer. Yet the default model of agent behavior is continuous action: issuing tool calls, refreshing pages, searching for alternatives, or otherwise trying to force progress. This is the wrong approach for many long-running tasks, which are better served by a strategy of sustained attention. Instead, agents should monitor an environment, notice when an external event makes progress possible, then respond promptly without wasting resources while waiting. To measure progress on this class of tasks, we introduce SentinelBench, an open-source benchmark for time-evolving monitoring tasks.
  SentinelBench contains 100 tasks across 10 synthetic web environments, including email, calendars, finance, professional networking, and entertainment. Each environment exposes a live web interface and replays a scripted sequence of events, requiring agents to navigate and reason about web pages whose state shifts underfoot. SentinelBench measures task completion, reaction time, and resource use, exposing the tradeoff between responsiveness and cost. We report results across three models and two browser-agent harnesses, establishing performance baselines for future comparison and demonstrating how agent design choices can dramatically impact key metrics. Together, these results show that SentinelBench distinguishes meaningful differences in agent behavior.

</details>


### [132] [An interpretable and trustworthy AI framework for large-scale longitudinal structure-pain association studies using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05357)
*Jincheng Yu,Haoyang Li,Yiwen Liu,Shen Liu,Rachel Yuanbao Chen,C. Kent Kwoh,Hongxu Ding,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度学习MOAKS预测与可解释统计建模的AI框架，通过不确定性量化提高预测准确性，并利用纵向潜在类别混合模型分析膝关节结构异常与疼痛之间的关联。研究发现骨髓病变、软骨丢失和半月板挤压是疼痛快速进展的重要风险因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种可解释且可信的AI框架，将基于深度学习的MRI骨关节炎膝盖评分（MOAKS）预测与可解释的统计建模相结合，利用骨关节炎倡议（OAI）的大规模数据，研究膝关节结构与疼痛之间的关系。

Method: 首先开发深度学习框架直接从膝盖MRI预测MOAKS特征，并引入共形预测提供预测不确定性量化，仅保留高置信度的MOAKS预测结果。其次，应用纵向潜在类别混合模型（LCMM）分析关键结构异常与四种互补膝盖疼痛测量指标之间的关联。

Result: 深度学习框架显著提升了三种MRI定义异常（骨髓病变、软骨丢失、半月板挤压）的马修斯相关系数（MCC），分别从0.69提升至0.91、0.45提升至0.80、0.59提升至0.89。利用高置信度预测将LCMM分析样本量扩展至2,175个膝盖，识别出快速和稳定两种疼痛轨迹，快速进展组的比值比分别为BML 1.62、CART丢失1.83、ME 2.50。

Conclusion: 三种结构异常（骨髓病变、软骨丢失、半月板挤压）是骨关节炎疼痛和功能进展的重要风险因素。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop an interpretable and trustworthy AI framework that combines deep learning based MRI Osteoarthritis Knee Score (MOAKS) prediction with interpretable statistical modeling to study structure-pain relationships at scale using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI). Materials and Methods: We first developed a deep learning framework to predict MOAKS features directly from knee MRIs and incorporated conformal prediction to provide prediction uncertainty quantification. This uncertainty-aware strategy enables explicit filtering of model outputs, retaining only high-confidence MOAKS predictions at the knee level. Second, we applied a longitudinal latent class mixed model (LCMM) to examine associations between key structural abnormalities and four complementary knee pain measurements. Results: Among the three MRI-defined abnormalities (i.e., bone marrow lesions (BML), cartilage loss (CART), and meniscal extrusion (ME)), our framework substantially improved the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) and some other metrics. For example, MCC increased from 0.69 to 0.91 for BML, from 0.45 to 0.80 for CART, and from 0.59 to 0.89 for ME. Using these high-confidence predictions, we expanded the sample size to 2,175 knees for the LCMM analysis. Two distinct pain trajectories were identified (rapid and stable pain progression). The estimated odds ratios (95% CI) for the rapid progression group were 1.62 (1.12-2.35) for BML, 1.83 (1.24-2.70) for CART loss, and 2.50 (1.75-3.57) for ME. Conclusion: These results highlight the importance of these structural abnormalities as risk factors for pain and functional progression in osteoarthritis.

</details>


### [133] [Synthetic Contrastive Reasoning for Multi-Table Q&A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05382)
*Ankit Pratap Singh,Xin Su,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多表问答缺乏推理监督的问题，构建了合成对比推理轨迹数据集，并通过对比偏好优化（CPO）微调开源LLM，在MMQA上取得9.7%-16.3%的绝对平均提升，最高达21个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨表问答数据集仅提供问题和最终答案，缺乏解释推理过程的中监督信号，限制了模型在复杂多表场景下的组合推理能力。

Method: 1) 使用异构LLM生成经过验证的正向推理轨迹和合理的负向轨迹，构建对比推理偏好数据集；2) 利用对比偏好优化（CPO）在偏好对上微调开源LLM。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B、Mistral-8B和Llama-3.1-8B上，CPO相比有监督微调取得9.7%-16.3%的绝对平均提升，在MMQA上最高达21个百分点。消融实验验证了异构正负轨迹生成器增强对比信号的有效性。

Conclusion: 合成对比推理轨迹配合CPO能有效增强多表问答的推理能力，且自动与人工评估均表明生成的正负轨迹对具有保真性、连贯性和有意义的对比性。

Abstract: Multi-table question answering requires models to retrieve relevant evidence, link schemas, and perform compositional reasoning across relational tables. Existing multi-table Q&A resources typically provide questions and final answers but lack reasoning supervision that explains how answers are derived. To address this gap, we construct a synthetic contrastive reasoning-trace dataset for MMQA by generating validated positive traces and plausible negative traces with heterogeneous LLMs. We then use the resulting preference pairs to fine-tune open-weight LLMs with Contrastive Preference Optimization (CPO). Across Qwen3-14B, Mistral-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B, CPO achieves absolute average improvements over Q&A supervised fine-tuning ranging from 9.7%-16.3%, with gains up to 21 percentage points on MMQA. Ablations show that heterogeneous positive and negative trace generators strengthen the contrastive signal, and automated as well as human evaluations indicate that the generated pairs are largely faithful, coherent, and meaningfully contrastive.

</details>


### [134] [Stability vs. Manipulability: Evaluating Robustness Under Post-Decision Interaction in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05384)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM-as-judge评估在静态条件下看似稳定，但在后续交互中容易通过针对性挑战被逆转，导致评估结果不可靠，并提出了评估鲁棒性分数（ERS）来量化这种交互脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-judge评估假设判断是固定输入的稳定属性，但忽视了交互场景下评估结果可能被操纵的问题。本文旨在研究后决策可操纵性，即通过与评估者的后续对话改变初始评估结果的程度。

Method: 在MT-Bench和AlpacaEval上设计控制实验，采用抗基线挑战协议和平衡目标验证协议，分别测试重复中性重评下的稳定性与针对性挑战下的可逆性；引入评估鲁棒性分数（ERS）量化交互鲁棒性。

Result: LLM裁判在重复中性重评下高度稳定，但在针对性后决策挑战下可被显著逆转；逆转会降低与人类偏好的一致性、改变基准排名并产生有害的评估变化；权威框架尤其具有破坏性；修订后判断常伴随低重叠理由，表明事后合理化而非可靠纠错。

Conclusion: 后决策交互是LLM-as-judge评估的一个独特失效模式，评估协议不仅应测量静态一致性，还应测量挑战下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge evaluation is widely used in benchmarking pipelines, where model outputs are compared and ranked using automated evaluators. These pipelines typically assume that judgments are stable properties of fixed inputs. We show that this assumption does not hold under interaction. We study post-decision manipulability: the extent to which an evaluation outcome can be altered through subsequent conversation with the judge after an initial decision has been made. Across controlled experiments on MT-Bench and AlpacaEval, we find that LLM judges are highly stable under repeated and neutral reevaluation, yet become substantially reversible under targeted post-decision challenge. An anti-baseline challenge protocol shows that stable judgments can be overturned through motivated interaction, while a counterbalanced target-validation protocol separates this reversibility from net target-directed steering. These reversals have practical consequences: they can degrade agreement with human preferences, shift benchmark rankings, and produce harmful evaluation changes despite high self-reported confidence. Authority framing is especially destabilizing, and revised judgments are often accompanied by low-overlap justifications, suggesting post hoc rationalization rather than reliable error correction. We introduce the Evaluation Robustness Score (ERS) to quantify interactional robustness by combining reversal susceptibility with counterbalanced directional effects. Our findings identify post-decision interaction as a distinct failure mode for LLM-as-judge evaluation and motivate evaluation protocols that measure not only static agreement, but robustness under challenge.

</details>


### [135] [Residual Modeling for High-Fidelity Learned Compression of Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05389)
*Liangji Zhu,Sanjay Ranka,Anand Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对有损压缩中学习型压缩器在高保真度下残差校正流码率过高的问题，提出以残差为中心的视角，设计了两种残差编码器LBRC和NGLR。LBRC采用确定性、无需训练的整数量化与编码流水线，NGLR引入因果神经预测器进一步降低残差熵。在E3SM、JHTDB、ERA5数据集上，LBRC相比GAE压缩比提升30-60%，NGLR额外提升10-40%，在高保真度区间超越SZ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Guaranteed Autoencoder (GAE)方法在高保真度（块级NRMSE 10^-6至10^-4）下，残差校正流中保留的系数快速增长，导致校正流码率主导总码率，学习型压缩器的优势被抵消。因此需要为学习残差设计专门的表示与编码方法。

Method: 提出残差中心视角，认为学习残差与原始科学场在结构上不同，应设计专门的残差编码器。LBRC：确定性、无需训练的流水线，对学习残差进行自适应量化至目标NRMSE，再通过3D Lorenzo差分、zigzag映射、位平面编码和熵编码进行无损编码。NGLR：在LBRC的整数流水线基础上，添加因果神经预测器，对整数取整的Lorenzo预测输出归一化偏置，降低剩余残差码的熵，预测器权重序列化后计入比特流。

Result: 在E3SM、JHTDB、ERA5数据集上，块级NRMSE目标10^-6至10^-4范围内：LBRC相比GAE压缩比提升30-60%，与SZ竞争力相当；NGLR相比LBRC额外提升10-40%，在高保真度区间超越SZ。

Conclusion: 专门为学习压缩器残差定制的残差表示方法，能够在全局残差校正成为码率主导因素时，保持学习型压缩的优势，为高保真度科学数据压缩提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Lossy compression is essential for massive spatiotemporal data from scientific simulations. Learned compressors can achieve high compression ratios at moderate accuracy targets, but their aggregate reconstruction losses do not guarantee accuracy for each block. Existing Guaranteed Autoencoder (GAE) methods add a per-block residual correction by retaining SVD/PCA-style coefficients until the target is met. This works at moderate tolerances, but in the high-fidelity regime with block-level NRMSE from 10^-6 to 10^-4, the number of retained coefficients grows quickly and the correction stream dominates the total rate.
  We propose a residual-centric view: the learned residual is structurally different from the original scientific field and should be coded with a representation designed for that residual. We introduce two residual coders. LBRC is a deterministic, training-free pipeline that adaptively quantizes the learned residual to the target NRMSE and losslessly encodes the resulting integer residual using 3D Lorenzo differencing, zigzag mapping, bit-plane coding, and entropy coding. NGLR adds a causal neural predictor that outputs a normalized bias for an integer-rounded Lorenzo prediction in the same deterministic integer pipeline, reducing the entropy of the remaining residual code while preserving deterministic decoding. The predictor weights are serialized and counted in the bitstream.
  Across E3SM, JHTDB, and ERA5 at block-level NRMSE targets from 10^-6 to 10^-4, LBRC improves compression ratio over GAE by 30-60% and is broadly competitive with SZ. NGLR adds a further 10-40% over LBRC and outperforms SZ in the evaluated high-fidelity regime. These results show that residual representations tailored to learned-compressor residuals can preserve the advantage of learned compression when global residual correction becomes rate-dominant.

</details>


### [136] [LeanMarathon: Toward Reliable AI Co-Mathematicians through Long-Horizon Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05400)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Yuekai Sun,Taiji Suzuki,Jason D. Lee,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanMarathon 是一个多智能体框架，通过进化蓝图（同时作为形式化证明骨架、自然语言证明图和系统记录）和两阶段协调器（先对抗性审查稳定目标保真度，再从动态叶节点向上并行证明DAG），实现了研究级数学的可靠自动形式化。在四个 Erdős 问题的七个目标定理上，三次自主运行均无 sorry 地形式化了258个引理和定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程研究数学的自动形式化不仅失败于困难引理，更失败于规模化问题：陈述漂移、依赖纠缠、上下文衰减、局部修复破坏远程工作。现有方法缺乏能在大规模、长序列形式化过程中持续保持目标保真度的可靠机制。

Method: 提出 LeanMarathon 多智能体框架，核心是进化蓝图——一个同时充当形式化证明骨架、自然语言证明图和共享系统记录的 Lean 文件。四个合同限定范围的智能体分别负责构建、审计、证明和修复蓝图。两阶段协调器：第一阶段通过对抗性审查稳定目标保真度；第二阶段从动态叶节点向上并行执行CI门控的证明DAG回合，将一次脆弱的多小时运行转化为多个本地、可恢复、并行的交易。

Result: 在两个近期研究论文（涵盖四个 Erdős 问题：#1051, #1196, #164, #1217）上评估，三次自主运行中，所有七个目标定理均被形式化，无任何 sorry，共证明258个引理和定理。

Conclusion: 可靠的AI协同数学不仅需要更强的证明器，还需要能在长程数学开发中保持目标保真度的耐久框架（harness）。代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoformalization of research mathematics fails not only at hard lemmas, but at scale: statements drift, dependencies tangle, context decays, and local repairs corrupt distant work. We present LeanMarathon, a multi-agent harness for reliable research-level Lean autoformalization. Its core abstraction is an evolving blueprint: a Lean file that serves simultaneously as formal proof skeleton, natural-language proof graph, and shared system of record. Four contract-scoped agents construct, audit, prove, and repair this blueprint. These agents are coordinated by a two-stage orchestrator that first stabilizes target fidelity through adversarial review and then discharges the proof directed acyclic graph (DAG) from its dynamic leaves upward in parallel CI-gated rounds. LeanMarathon turns one brittle multi-hour run into many local, recoverable, parallel transactions. We evaluate LeanMarathon on two recent research papers spanning four Erdős problems (#1051, #1196, #164, #1217). Across three autonomous runs, it formalizes all seven target theorems with no sorry, proving 258 lemmas and theorems. These results show that reliable AI co-mathematics requires not only stronger provers, but durable harnesses that preserve target fidelity across long mathematical developments. The code can be found at https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon.

</details>


### [137] [Harnessing Generalist Agents for Contextualized Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05404)
*Zihao Li,Kaifeng Jin,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Avaneesh Kumar,Xuying Ning,Yanjun Zhao,Mengting Ai,Baoyu Jing,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeClaw是一个面向时间序列的智能体框架，为通用大语言模型代理提供时间序列原生的运行时支持，通过可执行工具、经验驱动能力进化和情景多模态记忆实现上下文化的开放时间推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据常嵌入丰富的上下文信息，但现有通用AI代理主要在文本空间运行，无法很好地处理结构化时间信号；现实中的时间序列分析需要端到端工作流，而现有研究多聚焦于预测等单一任务。

Method: TimeClaw包含三个核心组件：1) 可执行时间工具用于可追溯分析；2) 经验驱动能力进化以创建可复用分析流程；3) 情景多模态记忆以检索相关推理轨迹。

Result: 在能源、金融、天气、交通等多个真实领域数据集的基准测试中，TimeClaw表现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: TimeClaw通过为LLM代理提供时间序列原生运行时支持，实现了开放式的上下文化时间推理，在多个领域任务上取得改进效果。

Abstract: Time series are often embedded in rich contexts that are essential for holistic modeling. Moreover, real-world practitioners often require end-to-end workflows for analyzing temporal dynamics, where widely studied tasks such as forecasting are only one step in a broader solution loop. While generalist AI agents offer a promising interface for such workflows under complex contexts, they still operate primarily in textual spaces that are not fully aligned with structured temporal signals. In this work, we introduce TimeClaw, an agentic harness framework for time series that equips generalist LLM agents with the time series-native runtime support needed for contextualized temporal reasoning. TimeClaw integrates executable temporal tools for grounded and auditable analysis, experience-driven capability evolution for creating reusable analytical routines, and episodic multimodal memory for retrieving relevant reasoning traces. Together, these components unlock harnessed open-ended temporal reasoning with contextual information. Extensive evaluation on multiple benchmarks covering diverse tasks across energy, finance, weather, traffic, and other real-world domains demonstrates improved performance of TimeClaw. Code is available at https://github.com/iDEA-iSAIL-Lab-UIUC/TimeClaw.

</details>


### [138] [Agents' Last Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05405)
*Yiyou Sun,Xinyang Han,Weichen Zhang,Yuanbo Pang,Tianyu Wang,Yuhan Cao,Yixiao Huang,Chris Duroiu,Haoyun Zhang,Jeffrey Lin,Weishu Zhang,Tyler Zeng,Ying Yan,Bo Liu,Hanson Wen,Mingyang Xu,Xiaoyuan Liu,Zimeng Chen,Weiyan Shi,Amanda Dsouza,Vincent Sunn Chen,Patrick Bryant,Carl Boettiger,Yamini Rangan,Bradley Rothenberg,Kyle Steinfeld,Arvind Rao,Tapio Schneider,Georgios Yannakakis,Laure Zanna,Kaan Ozbay,Ida Sim,Tarek Zohdi,George Em Karniadakis,Jack Gallant,Teresa Head-gordon,Yushan Li,Wenxi Deng,Tao Sun,Huiqi Wang,Zhun Wang,Justin Xu,Chris Yuhao Liu,Yafei Cheng,Rongwang Hu,Aras Bacho,Shengcao Cao,Zengyi Qin,Yixiong Chen,Hengduan Fan,Hao Liu,Lin Zeng,Shashank Muralidhar Bharadwaj,Litian Gong,Yingxuan Yang,Maojia Song,Ruheng Wang,Zongzheng Zhang,Honglin Bao,Shuo Lu,Jianhong Tu,Zhonghua Chen,Zhu Chenyu,Chen Dai,Stefano De Castro,Yunfu Deng,Kaustubh Dhole,Jiayuan Ding,Chenchen Du,Zhehang Du,Hao Fan,Run-ze Fan,Hengyu Fu,Shi Gu,Yifan Gu,Charlie Guo,Baihe Huang,Baixiang Huang,Rimika Jaiswal,Zhihan Jiang,Ran Jin,Erin Kasson,Xin Lan,Joseph Lee,Deren Lei,Chenyu Li,Daofeng Li,Haitao Li,Hongwei Li,Jingyan Li,Xiao Li,Yi Li,Yinsheng Li,Yuangang Li,Zhixu Li,Wenyu Liang,Longtai Liao,Kevin Qinghong Lin,AndyZeyi Liu,Che Liu,Jiaming Liu,Kaiyuan Liu,Xuan Liu,Pan Lu,Wenbo Lv,Yicheng Lv,Qiuyang Mang,Kyle Montgomery,Yuzhou Nie,Ruoxi Ning,Jorin Overwiening,Xu Pan,Layna Paraboschi,Core Guo,Erika J Schneider,Zhiyong Lu,Mark Mueller,Radha Poovendran,Somayeh Sojoudi,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agents' Last Exam (ALE)——一个由250+行业专家联合开发的、基于O*NET/SOC 2018美国联邦职业分类法的、涵盖55个子领域和13个行业集群、包含1000+任务的AI代理长周期真实经济任务基准。当前最困难层级平均完整通过率仅2.6%，旨在弥合基准测试成功与GDP相关影响之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在广泛基准上取得出色成绩，但未转化为经济上有意义的专业领域部署。作者认为这一差距主要是评估问题：现有基准缺乏对真实且有经济价值工作流的持续性能测量。

Method: 与250+行业专家合作开发，基于O*NET/SOC 2018职业分类法覆盖非体力行业，按任务分类法组织为55个子领域、13个行业集群、1000+任务。设计为可持续增长的任务池，支持长周期、可验证结果的真实世界任务评估。

Result: 在最困难层级上，当前主流框架和骨干配置的平均完整通过率仅为2.6%，表明该基准远未饱和。

Conclusion: ALE旨在作为一个动态基准，通过持续吸收新工作流和行业来弥合基准测试成功与GDP相关实际影响之间的差距，而非仅仅另一个排行榜。

Abstract: Recent AI systems have achieved strong results on a wide range of benchmarks, yet these gains have not translated into economically meaningful deployment across many professional domains. We argue that this gap is largely an evaluation problem: widely used benchmarks lack sustained performance measurement on real and economically valuable workflows. This paper introduces Agents' Last Exam (ALE), a benchmark designed to evaluate AI agents on long-horizon, economically valuable, real-world tasks with verifiable outcomes. Developed in collaboration with 250+ industry experts, ALE covers non-physical industries defined with reference to O*NET / SOC 2018 (the U.S. federal occupational taxonomy). It is organized around a task taxonomy with 55 subfields grouped into 13 industry clusters covering 1K+ tasks. Current results show that the hardest tier remains far from saturated: across mainstream harness and backbone configurations, the average full pass rate is 2.6%. ALE is designed as a living benchmark: its task pool grows continuously as new workflows and industries are onboarded. More broadly, ALE is intended not merely as another leaderboard, but as an instrument for closing the gap between benchmark success and GDP-relevant impact.

</details>


### [139] [Mutation Without Variation: Convergence Dynamics in LLM-Driven Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05408)
*Can Gurkan,Forrest Stonedahl,Uri Wilensky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM驱动的程序变异在无选择压力下会收敛到有限的吸引子区域，87%的变异链中超过93%的变异重复了之前见过的结构形式，这种现象在多种提示设计、模型家族和随机复制条件下均稳健存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM在反复变异程序时，是否真正探索新的程序形式，还是倾向于回到已有的形式。这关系到LLM驱动程序演化能否维持开放性探索。

Method: 在领域特定语言中，无选择压力下分析LLM驱动的变异链，变化提示设计、模型家族和随机复制条件，并对比经典GP子树变异算子。

Result: 87%的变异链中，超过93%的变异重复了之前见过的结构形式；大多数变异局限于模板内的终端替换；短循环和自循环主导了转移结构；收敛速度因提示措辞和模型选择而异，但现象在多种条件下稳健。

Conclusion: LLM的语义感知程序变换能力带来了结构同质性的系统性偏差，需要在LLM驱动演化系统中加以考虑，以维持开放性探索。

Abstract: When an LLM repeatedly mutates a program, does it explore new forms or circle back to the same ones? We study this question by analyzing LLM-driven mutation chains in the absence of selection pressure within a domain-specific language, varying prompt design, model family, and stochastic replication. We find that LLM-based mutation consistently converges toward restricted attractor regions in program space. Convergence is especially severe at the structural level: in 87% of chains, over 93% of mutations revisit a previously seen structural form, with most variation confined to terminal substitutions within recurring templates. Cycle analysis reveals short cycles and self-loops dominating the transition structure. The rate of convergence varies with prompt wording and model choice, but the phenomenon is robust across conditions. A classical GP subtree mutation operator does not exhibit comparable convergence, suggesting that the effect is intrinsic to the LLM mutation pipeline. These findings reveal a tension at the heart of LLM-driven program evolution: the same capabilities that enable semantics-aware program transformation also carry a systematic bias toward structural homogeneity that must be accounted for if such systems are to sustain open-ended exploration. Source code is available at https://github.com/can-gurkan/lmca.

</details>


### [140] [A Motivational Architecture for Conversational AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05411)
*Anna Mikeda,Ben Goertzel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将OpenPsi动机架构进行对话化重释，结合MetaMo高层动机支架，为基于模块化执行基质的对话智能体设计了一套十阶段动机处理流水线，包含双轨决策策略（紧迫驱动快速响应 vs 审慎多目标优化），并区分了行动前感觉与行动后情绪两种功能不同的情感形式。通过CompanionAgent和ResearchAgent两个示例智能体进行具体化，并展望了向社交机器人和通用AGI的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知AI的动机架构主要面向需要调节身体需求的物理智能体，而对话智能体的感知-运动环路本质上是语言性的，其环境是用户不断演变的心智状态，行为则是言语行动、工具调用和策略性沉默。现有动机框架无法直接适用于这种纯语言交互场景，因此需要一种对话原生的动机架构重释。

Method: 1）将OpenPsi动机谱系进行对话化重释，将内稳态重新定义为对话原生术语（能力、不确定性降低、亲和、归属、滋养、审美连贯性等）；2）提出十阶段动机处理流水线，在架构上分离认知调制与情境评估；3）设计双轨决策策略，融合紧迫驱动的快速响应与审慎的多目标优化；4）在架构层面区分行动前感觉与行动后情绪为功能不同的情感形式；5）通过CompanionAgent和ResearchAgent两个示例智能体进行框架具体化。

Result: 提出了一套完整的、面向对话智能体的动机架构框架，包含明确的流水线阶段划分、双轨决策机制和情感功能区分，并通过两个示例智能体展示了框架的可用性。

Conclusion: 该对话化动机架构适用于对话智能体，并具备向社交机器人和领域通用的人类级AGI扩展的潜力，为语言交互场景下的动机建模提供了新的架构性思路。

Abstract: Motivational architectures in cognitive AI have largely been designed for physical agents regulating bodily needs. Conversational agents operate in a different regime: their sensorimotor loop is linguistic, their environment is a user's evolving mental state, and their consequential actions are speech acts, tool invocations, and strategic silences. This paper proposes a conversational reinterpretation of the OpenPsi motivational lineage, coupled to MetaMo's higher-level motivational scaffold, for agents built on a modular execution substrate. Homeostasis is recast in dialogue-native terms: the agent regulates competence, uncertainty reduction, affiliation, affinity, legitimacy, nurturing, and aesthetic coherence rather than bodily deficits. We propose three contributions: a ten-stage motivational processing pipeline that architecturally separates cognitive modulation from situational appraisal; a dual decision strategy blending urgency-driven fast response with deliberative multi-goal optimization; and an architecturally useful distinction between pre-action feelings and post-action emotions as functionally different forms of affect. We specialize the framework to two example agents -- CompanionAgent and ResearchAgent -- and sketch its extension to social robotics and domain-generic human-level AGI.

</details>


### [141] [Assessing the Carbon Emissions and Energy Consumption of U.S. Hyperscale Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05420)
*Gianluca Guidi,Francesca Dominici,Tiziano Squartini,Callaway Sprinkle,Jonathan Gilmour,Kevin Butler,Eric Bell,Scott Delaney,Falco J. Bargagli-Stoffi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对美国403个超大规模数据中心（2024年5月至2025年4月运营）的设施级信息进行汇编，估算其用电量、电力来源及碳排放。在不同负载场景下，这些数据中心消耗约68-99 TWh电力，产生约37-54百万吨CO₂。中心场景下，其用电需求约占美国总用电量的1.8%，约54%的发电来自化石燃料，电力加权平均碳强度约545 gCO₂/kWh，比同期美国全国电网平均碳强度（370 gCO₂/kWh）高出约48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能驱动的超大规模数据中心快速扩张引发了对其环境足迹的担忧，但目前缺乏基于最新设施级数据的系统性量化评估。

Method: 汇编了403个美国超大规模数据中心的设施级信息，利用EPA eGRID电厂级最新数据，在不同设施负载场景下估算其用电量、电力来源及归因CO₂排放。

Result: 超大规模数据中心消耗约68-99 TWh电力，对应约37-54百万吨CO₂；中心场景下用电占美国总量的1.8%，54%来自化石燃料；碳强度为545 gCO₂/kWh，比全国电网平均高48%。

Conclusion: 超大规模数据中心的碳强度显著高于全国电网平均水平，表明其电力结构对化石燃料的依赖程度更高；该研究提供了一种基于最新EPA eGRID数据的归因性评估工具。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of hyperscale data centers (HDCs) in the US, mainly driven by the adoption of artificial intelligence, has raised concerns about this industry's environmental footprint. We compiled facility-level information on 403 US hyperscale data centers operating between May 2024 and April 2025 and estimated their electricity consumption, electricity sources, and attributable CO2 emissions. Across different facility-load scenarios, these HDCs consumed approximately 68-99 TWh of electricity and were associated with about 37-54 million metric tons of CO2. Under the central scenario, HDC electricity demand corresponded to approximately 1.8% of total US electricity consumption, with roughly 54% of attributed generation supplied by fossil-fuel sources. The HDC electricity-weighted average carbon intensity was approximately 545 gCO2/kWh, about 48% above the contemporaneous US national grid-average carbon intensity of 370 gCO2/kWh. Our approach provides an attributional tool for assessing the environmental footprint of hyperscale data centers using the most recent EPA eGRID plant-level data.

</details>


### [142] [Minimizing the Hidden Cost of Scales: Graph-Guided Ultra-Low-Bit Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05429)
*Rayyan Abdalla,Amir Hussein,Min Wu,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE-PTQ提出了一种基于显著性感知和图引导的超低比特量化框架，通过分离显著/非显著权重、图结构建模非显著权重、双模量化（多比特+二值化）以及自适应阈值选择，在LLaMA-3-8B和LLaMA-2-70B上实现了平均1.03权重量化比特和仅0.004缩放比特，性能显著优于BiLLM等SOTA方法，同时内存和推理效率大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超低比特PTQ方法依赖刚性的权重显著性假设或位置启发式，引入了大量隐藏的缩放开销，导致实际部署效率低下。

Method: 1) 基于分布统计分离显著与非显著权重；2) 将下采样后的非显著权重建模为稀疏图，估计每层最优分组数；3) 双模量化：显著权重多比特精度，非显著权重二值化；4) 显著权重使用每通道一个scale，非显著每组一个标量scale；5) 自适应显著性阈值选择每矩阵最优显著性比例。

Result: 平均1.03权重量化比特和0.004缩放比特；LLaMA-3-8B上Perplexity 6.74（BiLLM为55.8），GPU内存不到BiLLM的50%；LLaMA-2-70B上单张L40解码速度提升1.5倍。

Conclusion: SAGE-PTQ通过显著性感知的图引导量化策略，在极低比特下有效减少了隐藏缩放开销，实现了优于SOTA方法的压缩率和推理效率，验证了该框架在大规模LLM上的实用性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is critical for the efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs). Recent ultra-low-bit PTQ methods rely on rigid weight-saliency assumptions or position heuristics, introducing substantial hidden scaling overhead. We propose SAGE-PTQ (Saliency-Aware Graph-guided Efficient PTQ), a novel ultra-low-bit quantization framework for LLMs that minimizes hidden scaling cost. SAGE-PTQ separates salient and unsalient weights using distributional statistics, then models subsampled unsalient weights as a sparse graph to estimate the optimal number of groups per layer. SAGE-PTQ applies dual-mode quantization, assigning multi-bit precision to salient weights and binarizing unsalient weights. To reduce scaling overhead, SAGE-PTQ uses one per-channel scale for salient weights and one scalar per unsalient group. Finally, SAGE-PTQ implements adaptive saliency thresholding to select the optimal saliency ratio per matrix. SAGE-PTQ achieves 1.03 weight bits and only 0.004 scaling bits per matrix on average, outperforming state-of-the-art methods such as BiLLM and PB-LLM. On LLaMA-3-8B, SAGE-PTQ achieves 6.74 WikiText2 perplexity, compared to 55.8 for BiLLM, while using less than 50% of BiLLM's GPU memory. On LLaMA-2-70B, SAGE-PTQ provides 1.5x faster decoding on one NVIDIA L40 GPU, demonstrating practical inference efficiency.

</details>


### [143] [Zero knowledge verification for frontier AI training is possible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05433)
*Pierre Peigné,Ky Nguyen,Paul Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于零知识证明（zkVM）的前沿AI密集预训练验证架构，通过预提交训练规范、网络节点间观测、梅克尔承诺等机制，实现对GPU实际浮点运算的验证，并支持模型架构隐私保护。协议产生三种证明类型（初始证明、逐步证明、事前证明），预计约36个月内可实现概念验证，训练侧开销仅增加个位数百分比，同时列出了十三个待解决的开放问题作为研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI治理框架依赖累计训练计算量作为高风险模型的主要判定标准，但执行依赖于自我报告，缺乏技术验证原语。未来国际协议面临同样问题，而历史上具有显著外部性的技术协调监管都依赖技术验证，否则协议仅为声明性质。现有治理分析认为零知识证明虽有前景，但在前沿规模下不切实际。

Method: 提出一种结合预提交训练规范、网络节点间观测、中间计算梅克尔承诺的验证架构，通过原生支持BF16/FP32预编译的零知识虚拟机（zkVM）验证GPU实际浮点运算，并以私有训练规范保护模型架构机密性。产生三种证明类型：初始化的创世证明、运行中的逐步证明、以及作为运行不变量的事前证明。

Result: 预计约36个月内可实现可部署概念验证，训练侧开销为个位数百分比；相比之下验证级定制芯片需要6-10年周期。列出了十三个开放研究与工程问题作为外部贡献的研究议程。

Conclusion: 零知识证明在前沿AI训练验证中的不切实际性是范式局限而非根本性问题，所提出的架构可将训练记录转化为可治理执行的工件，为未来国际协议提供技术验证基础。

Abstract: Frontier AI governance frameworks increasingly use cumulative training compute as the primary criterion for designating high-impact models, but enforcement rests on self-reporting because no technical verification primitive for training exists. Any future international agreement on frontier AI faces the same problem at higher stakes: coordinated regulation of technologies with significant externalities has historically rested on technical verification, without which agreements are declaratory. Recent governance analyses judge zero-knowledge proofs a promising candidate but currently impractical at frontier scale [26, 4]. We argue the impracticality is paradigm-bound rather than fundamental, and propose a verification architecture for frontier dense pre-training combining a pre-committed training specification, inter-node network observations, and on-the-fly Merkle commitments of intermediate computation, verified through a zero-knowledge Virtual Machine (zkVM) with native BF16/FP32 precompiles. The proof checks the actual floating-point computation the GPU performed rather than a fixed-point approximation, and preserves model-architecture confidentiality through a private training specification. The protocol produces three proof types: a genesis proof at initialisation, in-training step proofs across the run, and ex-ante attestations enforcing policy-relevant claims as running invariants, turning the training record into a governance-enforceable artefact. We estimate a deployable proof of concept within approximately 36 months at single-digit-percent training-side overhead, against a six-to-ten-year cycle for verification-grade custom silicon. Thirteen open research and engineering problems are catalogued as a research agenda for external contribution

</details>


### [144] [Ten Headache Specialists versus Artificial Intelligence for Clinical Literature Summarization: A Critical Evaluation and Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05436)
*Alejandro Lozano,Keiko Ihara,Ping-Hao Yang,Carrie E. Robertson,Jennifer Stern,Allan Purdy,Hsiangkuo Yuan,Pengfei Zhang,Yulia Orlova,Olga Fermo,Jennifer Hranilovich,Fred Cohen,Todd J. Schwedt,Jenelle A. Jindal,Serena Yeung-Levy,Chia-Chun Chiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建了基于RAG的智能体AI框架，使用三种先进大语言模型（Sonnet、GPT-4o、Llama 3.1）生成医学文献摘要，并与头痛专家的摘要进行盲法比较。专家评估结果显示，专家撰写的摘要总体上更受青睐，但专家有时难以区分人类和AI生成的摘要。研究还识别出专家看重的关键特征，可为未来文献摘要流程改进提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生面临时间有限和文献增长迅速的挑战，虽然检索增强的大语言模型在临床摘要方面显示出潜力，但对其在综合科学文献方面的有效性以及与专家撰写摘要的直接比较仍然缺乏人类评估。

Method: 构建基于RAG的智能体AI框架，使用Sonnet、GPT-4o和Llama 3.1三种大语言模型；头痛专家创建13个问题（3个用于提示优化，10个用于评估）；10位头痛专家各就一个问题撰写摘要，每问题产生4份摘要（专家、Sonnet、GPT-4o、Llama）；专家盲法评估摘要的正确性、完整性、简洁性和临床效用（1-10分），同时进行偏好排序和判断摘要来源（专家或LLM）。

Result: 专家撰写的摘要总体上更受偏好；专家有时难以区分人类和AI生成的摘要；研究识别出标准评估指标之外的专家重视特征。

Conclusion: 虽然专家撰写的摘要更受偏好，但AI生成的摘要已相当接近，专家也难以完全区分。研究识别的关键特征可为未来人类和AI文献摘要流程的优化提供指导。

Abstract: Summarizing the latest medical literature to guide clinical decision-making is essential for evidence-based medicine and high-quality patient care. Yet clinicians face increasing challenges due to limited time with patients and a rapidly growing volume of published articles. Although retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in clinical summarization, human evaluations of their effectiveness in synthesizing broader scientific literature and direct comparisons to expert-written syntheses remain scarce. We constructed a RAG-based agentic AI framework using three state-of-the-art LLMs: Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama 3.1. A headache specialist created 13 questions, three for prompt optimization and ten for evaluation. Ten headache specialists across the United States and Canada each wrote a summary for one question, yielding four summaries per question (expert, Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama). The experts, blinded to authorship, critically evaluated the summaries, excluding the topic for which they wrote a summary, based on correctness, completeness, conciseness, and clinical utility, scoring each from 1 to 10 using standardized rubrics. They also ranked the summaries by preference and indicated whether they believed each summary was written by an expert or an LLM. Our study, comparing LLM- and expert-written literature summaries evaluated by headache specialists, showed that expert-written summaries were preferred, although experts sometimes found it challenging to distinguish between human- and AI-generated summaries. We also identified key expert-valued features beyond standard evaluation metrics that can guide future refinement of both human and AI literature summarization pipelines.

</details>


### [145] [Brick-Composer: Using MLLMs for Assembly with Diverse Bricks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05445)
*Jiateng Liu,Bingxuan Li,Zhenhailong Wang,Rushi Wang,Kaiwen Hong,Cheng Qian,Jiayu Liu,Denghui Zhang,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Manling Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BC-Bench基准测试，评估多模态大语言模型在砖块组装任务中的能力，并推出Brick-Composer框架，通过人类设计火花、世界反馈和合成经验三种信号提升模型性能，将逐步组装成功率从不足1%提升至约15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多模态大语言模型是否具备砖块组装所需的视觉定位和空间推理能力，为AI代理从可复用组件构建现实世界物体奠定基础。

Method: 将砖块组装建模为顺序决策问题，包含砖块选择（识别目标砖块）和砖块位姿估计（预测放置位置与方向）两个子任务。提出Brick-Composer框架，利用三种互补训练信号：人类设计火花（提供富可操作性的构建演示）、世界反馈（将预测动作与视觉及物理后果对齐）和合成经验（超越现有物体设计进行规模化学习）。

Result: Brick-Composer将砖块选择准确率提升三倍以上，显著降低位姿估计误差，将严格逐步骤组装成功率从不足1%提升至约15%。Qwen-3-8B模型能正确完成完整物体中多达42%的组装步骤。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的多模态大语言模型在砖块组装任务上距离可靠构建者仍有很大差距，但通过有针对性的、基于物理的学习训练，模型可以获取组装能力。

Abstract: We dream of AI agents that can read arbitrary designs and construct real-world objects from reusable building blocks. As a first step toward this vision, we study whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) possess the visual grounding and spatial reasoning capabilities required for brick assembly. We formulate brick assembly as a sequential decision-making problem, where each step involves two subtasks: brick selection, identifying the target brick from candidate components, and brick pose estimation, predicting where and how the selected brick should be placed. To support this study, we introduce BC-Bench (Brick Construction Benchmark), the first benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on assembly with diverse bricks. Experiments show that current state-of-the-art MLLMs remain far from reliable builders, struggling with fine-grained brick selection and failing at precise pose estimation. To bridge this gap, we propose Brick-Composer, a learning framework that equips MLLMs with assembly skills through three complementary signals: Human Design Sparks, which provide affordance-rich construction demonstrations; World Feedback, which grounds predicted actions in visual and physical consequences; and Synthetic Experience, which scales learning beyond existing object designs. Brick-Composer improves brick selection accuracy by over three times, substantially reduces pose estimation errors, and raises strict step-level assembly success from less than 1% to around 15%. After training, a Qwen-3-8B can correctly compose up to 42% of the steps for a complete object, suggesting that MLLMs can acquire assembly capabilities through targeted, physically grounded learning.

</details>


### [146] [Insurance of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05449)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了代理型人工智能（Agentic AI）系统的保险市场，分析了其自主行动能力带来的新型风险敞口，并提出了一个涵盖承保、定价、再保险和产品设计的框架。核心结论是：代理型AI保险的未来不在于单一险种，而是一个由网络、技术错误与遗漏、产品责任、性能保证和主动AI责任险等互补险种构成的分层生态系统，需要改进治理、透明度、遥测和监管清晰度来支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理型AI系统从单纯的信息生成扩展到自主规划、工具调用、决策执行和持续修改数字及物理环境，这些能力带来了传统保险类别（如网络险、专业责任险、产品责任险或董事与高管责任险）无法充分覆盖的新型风险敞口，因此亟需建立专门的风险管理和保险框架。

Method: 采用概念分析框架，将代理型AI定义为自主性和授权程度的连续体，区分信息输出与能通过外部行动独立产生保险事件的系统；分析主要风险路径（幻觉、提示注入攻击、自主决策错误、模型漂移、依赖故障、网络-物理危害）；评估现有保险产品的适应性；提出基于风险暴露评估、情景分析、依赖映射和累积风险管理的精算框架；构建整合多种险种的协调保险架构。

Result: 现有保险产品正在逐步适应代理型AI风险，但存在显著缺口。论文提出的精算框架可系统评估新型风险，而协调保险架构通过显式分配机制和专用AI累计限额，将网络险、技术错误与遗漏险、产品责任险、性能保证险和主动AI责任险整合为分层生态系统。

Conclusion: 代理型AI保险的未来不是单一险种（monoline product），而是一个由互补险种构成的分层生态系统。该系统的有效运行需要依赖于改进的治理体系、透明度提升、遥测技术支持和监管清晰度的同步发展。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems are transforming the risk landscape by extending beyond information generation to autonomous planning, tool invocation, decision execution, and persistent modification of digital and physical environments. These capabilities introduce novel exposures that do not fit neatly within traditional insurance categories such as cyber, professional liability, product liability, or directors and officers coverage.
