Conclusion: TokenMizer以一半的标记成本提供了可查询的替代方案，优于纯文本保留基线。其在保留结构化决策信息方面具有显著优势，但性能因领域异质性而波动（软件工程等显式命令式表述得分高于研究等隐式推理场景）。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) deployments for long-horizon tasks face a fundamental constraint: context windows are finite while productive work sessions are not. When history exceeds the Maximum Effective Context Window (MECW), critical structured information - architectural decisions, task transitions, file histories - is silently discarded. Existing mitigations treat history as flat text, destroying the relational structure that makes sessions resumable. We present TokenMizer, an open-source proxy system that models LLM session history as a typed knowledge graph. The schema defines 14 node types and 7 edge types. A hybrid extraction pipeline populates the graph incrementally, while a three-tier checkpoint system serializes it into compact resume blocks. An 8-layer compression pipeline reduces context overhead, and a semantic cache reduces repeated-query latency. Evaluated on a controlled benchmark of 21 sessions spanning 5 domains, TokenMizer demonstrates significant token economy. It produces resume blocks averaging 78 tokens (range: 42-124) - 2x smaller than evaluated baselines (159-170 tokens) - while achieving higher decision recall (+9-17 percentage points). Crucially, baselines only preserve that a technology was mentioned; TokenMizer preserves the rationale. Across all sessions, TokenMizer achieves mean task recall 51.0%, decision recall 46.6%, and file recall 58.7%. Variance reflects domain heterogeneity: explicit imperative phrasing (software engineering) scores higher than implicit reasoning (research). Ablation studies show fuzzy label matching is the dominant improvement factor (+33 pp task recall). The heuristic compression achieves 47.3% token reduction with zero external dependencies. TokenMizer provides a queryable alternative to text-retention baselines at half the token cost.

</details>


### [222] [Boosting Brain-to-Image Decoding with TRIBE v2 Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06345)
*Yohann Benchetrit,Marlène Careil,Simon Dahan,Hubert Banville,Stéphane d'Ascoli,Jean-Rémi King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探究能否通过预训练的fMRI编码模型(TRIBE v2)生成的合成数据来增强小样本fMRI数据集，从而提升脑解码性能。在两个数据集(NSD 7T和BOLD5000 3T)上，图像解码Top-10检索准确率最高提升68%，且纯合成数据训练的解码器在某些设置下也能超越随机水平，表明大规模fMRI编码模型可支持零样本脑到图像解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑解码受限于标注神经数据的稀缺性，在低数据场景下表现不佳。因此本文研究能否利用大规模预训练fMRI编码模型生成的合成数据来增强小样本数据集，提升脑解码的数据效率。

Method: 使用预训练的大规模fMRI编码模型TRIBE v2（在>1000小时fMRI响应数据上训练，覆盖视频、音频和语言刺激）生成合成fMRI数据。在NSD 7T和BOLD5000 3T两个数据集上，系统地评估图像解码器性能随合成数据量变化的网格，对比纯真实数据训练、混合数据训练和纯合成数据训练的效果。

Result: 混合合成数据训练可将Top-10图像检索准确率最高提升68%（相比仅用真实数据）；需根据数据源调整合成数据比例以达到最佳解码性能；纯合成数据训练的解码器在某些设置下也能高于随机水平，实现零样本脑到图像解码。

Conclusion: 大规模fMRI响应模型（覆盖视觉、听觉和语言刺激）可作为基础，通过生成合成数据显著提升图像解码的数据效率，甚至支持零样本解码。

Abstract: Brain decoding is limited by the availability of labeled neural data, and remains challenging in low-data regimes. To address this issue, we investigate whether and when brain decoding can be boosted by augmenting small fMRI datasets with synthetic data generated by a pretrained model of fMRI responses to stimuli. We use TRIBE v2, a large encoding model pretrained on more than 1000 hours of fMRI responses to video, audio and language. For each dataset, we evaluate systematic grids that show how the performance of image decoders varies with the amount of synthetic data used for training. Our results, based on two datasets (the 7T fMRI Natural Scenes Dataset and 3T fMRI BOLD5000), show up to 68% improvement in Top-10 image-retrieval accuracy compared to decoders trained only on real data. Importantly, the proportion of augmented data required to reach a given image decoding performance needs to be adjusted depending on the data source. Surprisingly, image decoders trained exclusively on synthetic fMRI can perform above chance in some settings, suggesting that TRIBE v2 can support zero-shot brain-to-image decoding. Together, these results show how large-scale models of the fMRI responses to sight, sound and language may provide a foundation to improve the data efficiency for image decoding.

</details>


### [223] [Where Should Knowledge Enter? A Layered Framework for Knowledge Infusion in Multimodal Iterative Generative Mo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06356)
*Renjith Prasad,Chathurangi Shyalika,Anushka Pawar,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出知识注入迭代生成模型是一个干预层问题，将知识注入分为四个层：表面层、轨迹层、潜变量层和参数层。在扩散模型的安全对齐实验中，累积实现三层干预，将违反知识的输出减少了70.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法按技术分类而非生成过程组件分类，缺乏统一框架来理解知识注入的本质。作者认为知识注入本质上是干预生成轨迹中不同组件的问题。

Method: 提出四层干预框架：表面层（输入/输出边界）、轨迹层（转移函数）、潜变量层（中间状态）、参数层（模型参数）。在扩散模型中实例化，并在多模态知识图谱安全对齐实验中实现表面层（输入侧和输出侧）和轨迹-潜变量层（生成中途）的累积干预。

Result: 每一层额外干预能解决前一层无法覆盖的失败类别，与基线相比，违反知识的输出减少了70.97%，实证验证了框架的互补性预测。

Conclusion: 知识注入应被视为干预层问题，不同层具有互补性，多层组合能更全面地约束生成过程，提高知识合规性。

Abstract: Multimodal generative models produce fluent outputs but remain unreliable when generation must respect structured, domain-specific, or safety-critical knowledge. Existing methods incorporate knowledge through mechanisms such as prompt augmentation, guidance, latent editing, or fine-tuning, yet they are typically categorized by technique rather than by the component of the generative process they modify. We argue that knowledge infusion in iterative generative models is fundamentally anintervention-layer problem. Since thegenerative process unfolds as a trajectory of internal states, knowledge can act on four structurally distinct components of this process: the input/output boundary, the transition function, the intermediate state, and the model parameters. This maps to four intervention layers: surface, trajectory, latent, and parametric infusion. We instantiate the framework in diffusion models, map representative methods to all four layers, and derive design principles for multi-layer composition. In a controlled safety-alignment experiment using a multimodal knowledge graph with two diffusion backbones, we implement three of the four layers cumulatively, surface (input-side and output-side) and trajectory--latent (mid-generation). We show empirically that each additional layer addresses failure classes that prior layers cannot reach, reducing knowledge-violating outputs by 70.97% compared to vanilla generation and empirically confirming the framework's complementarity prediction.

</details>


### [224] [An Infectious Disease Spread Simulation Based on Large Language Model Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06360)
*Yonchanok Khaokaew,Ruochen Kong,Andreas Zufle,Hao Xue,Taylor Anderson,Chandini Raina MacIntyre,Matthew Scotch,Flora D. Salim,David J Heslop*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于LLM的空间感知智能体仿真框架，将LLM决策与人口普查数据结合，模拟流感样疾病的自报行为，发现收入和受教育程度是报告率变化的主要驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病爆发期间个体决策建模对理解行为动态和制定有效干预措施至关重要，但现有研究缺乏将LLM决策与空间地理分布相结合的仿真框架。

Method: 构建基于智能体的空间仿真框架，将LLM生成的流感样疾病自报决策集成到基于人口普查的合成人群中。智能体被分配到城市内空间单元，捕获不同人口统计群体的空间分布。比较三种决策场景：独立推理、家庭影响和消息框架，在旧金山和亚特兰大进行模拟。

Result: 收入和受教育程度是报告率变化的主导驱动因素，地理因素、LLM模型选择和消息框架的影响较小但一致。该框架生成的合成数据能够捕获社会和地理异质性。

Conclusion: 该框架支持空间流行病学建模和基于偏差的行为分析，为理解传染病爆发期间个体决策的空间异质性提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Modelling individual decision-making during infectious disease outbreaks is crucial for understanding behavioural dynamics and informing effective public health interventions. Prior work has shown that large language models can simulate realistic human behaviour by generating agent decisions based on demographic prompts and situational context. We build on this foundation with a spatially grounded, agent-based simulation framework that integrates LLM-generated decisions about self-reported influenza-like illness into a census-based synthetic population of agents. Location is treated as a central feature: agents are assigned to spatial units within cities, capturing the spatial distributions of different demographic groups using real-world census data and enabling geographically diverse behavioural modelling. We implement and compare three decision scenarios, independent reasoning, household influence, and message framing, and simulate self-reporting outcomes in San Francisco and Atlanta. Results reveal that income and education are the dominant drivers of reporting rate variation, with smaller but consistent effects from geography, LLM model choice, and message framing. Our framework generates synthetic data that captures both social and geographic heterogeneity, supporting spatial epidemiological modelling and bias-aware behavioural analysis.

</details>


### [225] [Rethinking Infrastructure Inspection as Image Difference Classification: A Traffic Sign Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06375)
*Ching Yau Fergus Mok,Lavindra de Silva,Varun Kumar Reja,Ioannis Brilakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将图像缺陷检测重新定义为图像差异分类（IDC），利用数字孪生中资产状态连续监控的关系特性，以减少对标注数据的依赖。在低资源交通标志检测案例中，基于指令的分类器优于编码器方法，且参考图像对比有助于提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生在道路基础设施检测中受限于标注数据不足，因此需要一种能降低数据依赖的缺陷检测方法。

Method: 将图像缺陷检测重新表述为图像差异分类（IDC），利用资产状态连续监控的关系特性，并构建了新的高质量数据集，对比了基于指令的分类器与基于编码器的分类器。

Result: 基于指令的分类器在低资源交通标志检测任务中优于基于编码器的分类器，且参考图像对比有助于提升性能。

Conclusion: 图像差异分类（IDC）是一种有效应对基础设施检测中数据约束的任务建模方式，可用于数字孪生资产状态更新。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) allow the digitalization of road infrastructure inspection, though this is hindered by limited annotated data. This work exploits the relational nature of continuous asset condition monitoring to reformulate image-based defect detection as image difference classification (IDC) to reduce data reliance. This was evaluated in a case study on low-resource traffic sign inspection with different IDC classifiers using a newly-curated, high quality dataset. Results indicate that the instruction-based classifier outperforms encoder-based ones and gains from comparison with reference images. This shows that IDC can be an effective task modeling for tackling data constraints in infrastructure inspection and DT asset condition updating.

</details>


### [226] [Humans' ALMANAC: A Human Collaboration Dataset of Action-Level Mental Model Annotations for Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06388)
*Jiaju Chen,Yuxuan Lu,Jiayi Su,Chaoran Chen,Songlin Xiao,Zheng Zhang,Yun Wang,Yunyao Li,Jian Zhao,Tongshuang Wu,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ALMANAC是一个包含2,987个协作动作及动作级心智模型标注的数据集，用于评估LLM代理在人类协作中模拟行为和推断心智模型的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代理主要针对任务完成优化，缺乏在协作过程中持续维护和对齐自身推理、合作伙伴意图及共享目标的心智模型能力，且社区缺乏带有动作级心智模型标注的真实人类协作数据。

Method: 基于社会科学中的Map Task双人路由任务构建数据集，包含2,987个协作动作，每个动作配有理论驱动的心智模型标注（自我推理、感知的合作伙伴意图、感知的团队目标），并在六个LLM上进行了基准测试。

Result: 六个LLM在预测人类下一轮行为和心智模型的基准测试中，ALMANAC数据集有效评估了模型模拟人类协作行为和推断心智模型的能力。

Conclusion: ALMANAC数据集弥补了当前缺乏动作级心智模型标注的真实协作数据的空白，为提升LLM代理的过程级协作能力提供了重要基准资源。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents have enabled complex cognitive capabilities, such as multi-step reasoning, planning, and tool use, that increasingly position these agents as human collaborators. Effective collaboration, however, requires collaborators to continuously maintain and align mental models of their own reasoning,partners' intentions, and shared goals during the collaborative process. Today's agents rarely develop such capabilities since they are primarily optimized for task completion, and the community lacks authentic human collaboration data with action-level mental model annotations that could guide agents toward process-level collaborative competence. To bridge this gap, we present ALMANAC, a dataset of Action-Level Mental model ANnotations for Agent Collaboration built from the Map Task, a classic dyadic routing task from social science. ALMANAC contains 2,987 collaboration actions, each paired with theory-informed mental model annotations that record the participants' self-reasoning, perceived partner intent, and perceived team goal. We benchmark six LLMs on predicting humans' next-turn behavior and mental models. Our results demonstrate ALMANAC's utility in evaluating models' ability to simulate human collaborative behaviors and infer their underlying mental models.

</details>


### [227] [Risk Assessment of Autonomous Driving: Integrating Technical Failures, Ethical Dilemmas, and Policy Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06396)
*Boyi Chen,Shengqin Chu,Zicheng Wang,Brian Baetz,Zhen Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用NHTSA碰撞数据、加州DMV脱离报告、MIT道德机器数据集及五个司法管辖区的法规对比分析，发现自动驾驶技术主要故障模式是感知与分类错误，决策面临不同伦理框架冲突，各地区法规不一致增加了应用不确定性。建议采取整合工程标准、伦理讨论与制度监管的适应性协同治理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶技术在减少人为交通事故方面具有巨大潜力，但同时也带来了技术、伦理和法规层面的新型风险，亟需系统性评估。

Method: 基于NHTSA公共碰撞数据、加州DMV脱离报告、MIT道德机器数据集，以及对五个司法管辖区的法规比较分析。

Result: 主要技术故障模式为感知与分类错误，在报告事故中占比较大；存在不同的自动驾驶决策伦理框架；各地区法规不一致增加了大规模应用的不确定性。

Conclusion: 技术、伦理与法规问题紧密关联，需要协同解决，建议采取更适应性的合作治理方法，将工程标准、伦理讨论与制度监管相结合。

Abstract: Autonomous driving technology has the potential to reduce the large number of road traffic accidents caused by human error each year, but it also brings new types of risks that need to be evaluated from the aspects of technology, ethics and regulations. Based on public crash data from the National Highway Traffic Safety Administration (NHTSA), disengagement reports from the California Department of Motor Vehicles (DMV), the MIT Moral Machines dataset, and a comparative regulatory analysis of five jurisdictions, we have found that the main types of technical failure modes are perception and classification errors. These account for a relatively large proportion of the reported accidents, and it can be concluded that there are different ethical frameworks for autonomous vehicle decision-making, and inconsistent regulations in different areas increase the uncertainty of widespread application. Generally speaking, the problems of technology, ethics and regulation are closely related and need to be solved together. Therefore, this paper recommends a more adaptive and cooperative governance approach that combines engineering standards, ethical discussion, and institutional supervision.

</details>


### [228] [Unsupervised Skill Discovery for Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06416)
*Zhisong Qiu,Kangqi Song,Shengwei Tang,Shuofei Qiao,Lei Liang,Huajun Chen,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataCOPE是一个无监督的验证器引导的技能发现框架，通过从未标记的探索轨迹中提取验证信号，迭代协调数据分析智能体、无监督验证器和技能管理器，实现可复用数据分析技能的自动发现，在报告式和推理式分析任务上分别提升平均得分9.71%和32.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时技能增强提供了一种轻量级方式来改进数据分析智能体，但发现有效的数据分析技能仍然困难，因为可靠监督成本高昂且成功标准因分析格式而异。核心问题是如何仅从无标签探索中发现可复用的数据分析技能。

Method: DataCOPE包含三个迭代协调的组件：数据分析智能体（Data-Analytic Agent）生成轨迹、无监督验证器（Unsupervised Verifier）提取信号、技能管理器（Skill Manager）进行对比技能蒸馏。报告式分析采用自适应检查表验证器，推理式分析采用答案一致性验证器。

Result: 在Deep Data Research（报告式）和DABStep（推理式）任务上，DataCOPE一致优于基线方法。在四种模型设置下平均，报告式任务提升9.71%，推理式任务提升32.30%。

Conclusion: DataCOPE通过无监督验证器信号驱动的技能发现框架，有效解决了数据分析技能发现的监督瓶颈问题，验证了从无标签探索中提取可复用技能并将其泛化到未见任务的可行性。

Abstract: Inference-time skill augmentation provides a lightweight way to improve data-analytic agents by injecting reusable procedural knowledge without updating model parameters. However, discovering effective skills for data analysis remains challenging, as reliable supervision is expensive and success criteria vary across analytical formats. This raises the key question of how to discover reusable data-analysis skills from unlabeled exploration alone. We propose DataCOPE, an unsupervised verifier-guided skill discovery framework for data-analytic agents. DataCOPE derives verifier signals from the exploration trajectories and uses them to characterize relative quality or aggreement among trajectories. It iteratively coordinates a Data-Analytic Agent for trajectory generation, an Unsupervised Verifier for signal extraction, and a Skill Manager for contrastive skill distillation. For report-style analysis, we instantiate the verifier as an Adaptive Checklist Verifier that derives task-specific criteria, scores reports by verifiable coverage, and iteratively refines the checklist. For reasoning-style analysis, we instantiate it as an Answer Agreement Verifier that groups trajectories by answer agreement and uses self-consistency as an auxiliary signal. We evaluate DataCOPE on report-style analysis from Deep Data Research and reasoning-style analysis from DABStep. Across both settings, DataCOPE consistently improves held-out performance over baselines. Averaged across four model settings, DataCOPE improves the mean score by 9.71% and 32.30% on report-style and reasoning-style tasks respectively.

</details>


### [229] [Agent Memory: Characterization and System Implications of Stateful Long-Horizon Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06448)
*Yasmine Omri,Ziyu Gan,Zachary Broveak,Robin Geens,Zexue He,Alex Pentland,Marian Verhelst,Tsachy Weissman,Thierry Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM智能体记忆系统进行系统性特征分析，提出了面向系统的分类法、阶段感知的性能分析框架，并对10种代表性系统进行了基准测试，最终推导出10条系统设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体被越来越多地部署在需要长时间推理的任务上，但现有智能体记忆系统在系统级行为方面缺乏系统性特征分析，亟需理解不同设计选择对读写路径成本的影响。

Method: 1) 提出面向系统的四轴分类法；2) 构建阶段感知的性能分析框架，将成本归因于构建、检索和生成三个阶段；3) 在两种基准套件上对10个代表性系统进行特征分析。

Result: 揭示了设计选择如何在读写路径上转移成本，并推导出10条系统建议，涵盖构建调度、能力下限、查询量摊销、新鲜度-延迟权衡及集群规模管理等方面。

Conclusion: 智能体记忆系统的设计选择会显著影响读写路径的成本分布，通过系统级优化（如构建调度、查询摊销等）可以有效提升大规模部署下的性能。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed on long-horizon tasks requiring sustained reasoning over extended interaction histories. Realizing this at scale requires agents to persistently store, retrieve, and update their own memory across sessions. A rich ecosystem of agent memory systems has emerged spanning flat retrieval, LLM-mediated extraction, consolidating fact stores, and agentic control flows. Yet, their system-level behavior remains uncharacterized. We present the first systems characterization of agent memory. First, we introduce a system-oriented taxonomy classifying agent memory systems along four axes. Second, we build a phase-aware profiling harness attributing cost to construction, retrieval, and generation. Third, we characterize ten representative systems across two benchmark suites, uncovering how design choices shift cost across the write and read paths. Finally, we derive 10 system recommendations covering construction scheduling, capability floors, amortization via query volume, freshness-latency tradeoffs, and fleet-scale management.

</details>


### [230] [Vortex: Efficient and Programmable Sparse Attention Serving for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06453)
*Zhuoming Chen,Xinrui Zhong,Qilong Feng,Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Yang Zhou,Michael Qizhe Shieh,Zhihao Jia,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Vortex是一个结合Python前端语言和页式张量抽象的系统，能够快速原型设计、部署和评估多种稀疏注意力算法，并在现代LLM服务栈中实现显著的吞吐量提升（最高3.46倍于全注意力），同时支持新兴架构和超大规模模型（如MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash提升4.7倍，229B参数MiniMax-M2.7提升1.37倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生成长度不断增长，稀疏注意力变得越来越重要，但大规模部署和评估新的稀疏注意力算法在工程上非常繁琐，严重阻碍了人类研究人员和AI智能体探索稀疏注意力设计的效率。

Method: Vortex采用Python嵌入的前端语言配合以页为中心（page-centric）的张量抽象来表达广泛的稀疏注意力算法，后端紧密集成到现代LLM服务栈中，实现高效的稀疏注意力执行。

Result: AI智能体使用Vortex自动生成和优化多种算法，最佳方案吞吐量比全注意力高3.46倍且精度不变；在MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash上达4.7倍吞吐提升，在229B参数的MiniMax-M2.7上达1.37倍吞吐提升（NVIDIA B200 GPU）。

Conclusion: Vortex显著加速了稀疏注意力算法的设计与迭代周期，将理论效率增益有效转化为实际的吞吐量提升，并扩展了稀疏注意力在难以实验的新兴架构和超大模型上的适用性。

Abstract: Sparse attention is becoming increasingly important for serving large language models (LLMs) as generation lengths continue to grow. However, deploying and evaluating new sparse attention algorithms at scale remains highly engineering-intensive, slowing both human researchers and AI agents in exploring the sparse attention design. To address this challenge, we present Vortex, a system that combines a Python-embedded frontend language atop a page-centric tensor abstraction for expressing a broad range of sparse attention algorithms, with an efficient backend tightly integrated into modern LLM serving stacks. Vortex enables rapid prototyping, deployment, and evaluation of sparse attention algorithms, effectively translating their theoretical efficiency gains into real-world throughput improvements. As a result, Vortex substantially accelerates the design and iteration of sparse attention algorithms. First, AI agents use Vortex to automatically generate and refine diverse algorithms, the best reaching up to $3.46\times$ higher throughput than full attention while preserving accuracy. Second, Vortex extends sparse attention to emerging architectures and very large models that are otherwise hard to experiment with, reaching up to $4.7\times$ higher throughput on the MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash and $1.37\times$ on the 229B-parameter MiniMax-M2.7 on NVIDIA B200 GPUs.

</details>


### [231] [Benchmark Everything Everywhere All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06462)
*Shiyun Xiong,Dongming Wu,Peiwen Sun,Yuang Ai,Bokang Yang,Wencheng Han,Xiao-Hui Li,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Benchmark Agent，一个全自主智能体系统，能够从用户查询分析到数据标注和质量控制的完整流程自动化构建基准测试，并通过15个代表性基准验证其在文本理解、多模态理解和领域特定推理等场景中生成高质量样本的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试的构建劳动密集、难以复用，且发布后容易达到性能饱和，无法有效区分先进模型，面临可持续性和可扩展性挑战。

Method: 提出Benchmark Agent，一个全自主智能体框架，自动化完成基准构建全流程，包括用户查询分析、子任务设计、数据标注和质量控制。

Result: 在15个代表性基准上（涵盖文本理解、多模态理解、领域特定推理），通过人工评估、LLM-as-a-judge评估和一致性检查，证明该框架能以最小人工参与生成高质量基准样本。持续评估发现当前模型在特定领域推理任务上仍存在困难。

Conclusion: 快速演进的基准测试能显著助力研究社区，Benchmark Agent提供了一种可持续、可扩展的基准构建新范式。

Abstract: Benchmarks are fundamental for evaluating and advancing LLMs and MLLMs by providing standardized and explicit measures of performance. However, their construction is labor-intensive and hard to reuse, raising concerns about sustainability and scalability. Moreover, existing benchmarks often quickly reach performance saturation after their release, resulting in insufficient discrimination among state-of-the-art models. To address these challenges, we introduce Benchmark Agent, a fully autonomous agentic system designed for benchmark building. Our framework orchestrates the complete benchmark construction pipeline, from user query analysis and subtask design to data annotation and quality control. To assess Benchmark Agent, we implement it to produce 15 representative benchmarks, spanning diverse evaluation scenarios, including text understanding, multimodal understanding, and domain-specific reasoning. Extensive experiments, including human evaluation, LLM-as-a-judge assessment, and consistency checks, demonstrate Benchmark Agent can generate high-quality benchmark samples with minimal human involvement. More importantly, through continual evaluation, we observe several insightful findings, including that current models struggle with certain domain-specific reasoning tasks. We believe that rapidly evolving benchmarks can contribute significantly to the research community. The preview and code will be publicly available at the demo page and code repository.

</details>


### [232] [Goedel-Architect: Streamlining Formal Theorem Proving with Blueprint Generation and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06468)
*Jui-Hui Chung,Ziyang Cai,Zihao Li,Qishuo Yin,Rohit Agarwal,Simon Park,Rodrigo Porto,Narutatsu Ri,Ziran Yang,Shange Tang,Xingyu Dang,Hongzhou Lin,Mengdi Wang,Danqi Chen,Chi Jin,Liam H Fowl,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Goedel-Architect 是一个基于蓝图生成与精化的 Lean 4 形式化定理证明智能体框架。它首先生成形式化定义和引理的依赖图蓝图，然后并行证明每个未闭合引理，失败驱动蓝图精化。以 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 为骨干，在 MiniF2F-test 上达到 99.2% pass@1，PutnamBench 上 75.6% pass@1；结合自然语言证明引导，实现 MiniF2F-test 100%、PutnamBench 88.8%，并在 IMO 2025、Putnam 2025、USAMO 2026 上取得优异成绩，性能达开源 SOTA 且成本降低最高 500 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化定理证明方法多采用递归引理分解，容易陷入死胡同策略的低效循环。作者希望设计一种更高效的全局规划方案，通过蓝图引导和失败驱动的精化机制，提升大语言模型在形式化证明任务中的成功率。

Method: Goedel-Architect 包含两个核心阶段：1) 蓝图生成——构建从定义到主定理的依赖图，可接受自然语言证明作为引导；2) 并行证明与精化——工具增强的 Lean 证明器并行闭合每个开放引理节点，失败引理驱动全局蓝图的迭代精化。

Result: 使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash：MiniF2F-test 99.2% pass@1，PutnamBench 75.6% pass@1。加入自然语言引导后：MiniF2F-test 100%，PutnamBench 88.8%（597/672），IMO 2025 4/6，Putnam 2025 11/12，USAMO 2026 3/6。成本比同类开源管线低最高 500 倍。

Conclusion: Goedel-Architect 通过蓝图驱动、并行证明与失败精化机制，实现了开源形式化定理证明的最先进性能，同时在成本效率上具有显著优势，验证了全局规划策略在定理证明中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce Goedel-Architect, an agentic framework for formal theorem proving in Lean 4 centered on blueprint generation and refinement. A blueprint is a dependency graph of definitions and lemmas that builds up to the main theorem. First, Goedel-Architect generates a blueprint of formally stated definitions and lemmas, along with declared dependencies. This blueprint is optionally guided by a natural language proof. Then, a tool-equipped Lean prover component closes each open lemma node in parallel using relevant dependencies. Failed lemmas in turn drive refinement of the global blueprint. This strategy contrasts with other mainstream approaches which use recursive lemma decomposition, and can inefficiently loop on dead-end strategies. Using the open-weight DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B-A13B) as the backbone, Goedel-Architect attains 99.2% pass@1 on MiniF2F-test and 75.6% pass@1 on PutnamBench. With an optional natural-language proof seeding the initial blueprint on the harder problems, we additionally close the remaining two MiniF2F-test problems (reaching 100%), lift PutnamBench to 88.8% (597/672), and solve 4/6 on IMO 2025, 11/12 on Putnam 2025, and 3/6 on USAMO 2026. This represents state-of-the-art performance for an open-source pipeline at a price point up to 500x less than comparable open-source pipelines.

</details>


### [233] [MLEvolve: A Self-Evolving Framework for Automated Machine Learning Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06473)
*Shangheng Du,Xiangchao Yan,Jinxin Shi,Zongsheng Cao,Shiyang Feng,Zichen Liang,Boyuan Sun,Tianshuo Peng,Yifan Zhou,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MLEvolve 是一个基于LLM的自进化多智能体框架，通过渐进式MCGS、回溯记忆和分层控制，在MLE-Bench和数学算法优化任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLE智能体存在分支间信息隔离、无记忆搜索和缺乏层次控制的问题，限制了长期自进化能力。

Method: 1) 渐进式MCGS：通过图参考边实现跨分支信息流，结合熵驱动的渐进调度从广泛探索转向聚焦利用；2) 回溯记忆：冷启动知识库+动态全局记忆，支持任务特定经验的检索与重用；3) 解耦策略规划与代码生成，并引入自适应编码模式。

Result: 在MLE-Bench上12小时预算下（标准运行时的一半）取得多项SOTA（平均奖牌率、有效提交率）；在数学算法优化任务上超越AlphaEvolve等专用方法，展现强跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过跨分支信息融合、记忆积累和规划-生成解耦，MLEvolve有效提升了LLM智能体在长周期机器学习算法发现任务中的自进化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to long-horizon tasks such as scientific discovery and machine learning engineering (MLE), where sustained self-evolution becomes a key capability. However, existing MLE agents suffer from inter-branch information isolation, memoryless search, and lack of hierarchical control, which together hinder long-horizon optimization. We present MLEvolve, an LLM-based self-evolving multi-agent framework for end-to-end machine learning algorithm discovery. By extending tree search to Progressive MCGS, MLEvolve enables cross-branch information flow through graph-based reference edges and gradually shifts the search from broad exploration to focused exploitation with an entropy-inspired progressive schedule. To allow the agent to evolve with accumulated experience, we introduce Retrospective Memory, which combines a cold-start domain knowledge base with a dynamic global memory for task-specific experience retrieval and reuse. For stable long-horizon iteration, we further decouple strategic planning from code generation with adaptive coding modes. Evaluation on MLE-Bench shows that MLEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple dimensions including average medal rate and valid submission rate under a 12-hour budget (half the standard runtime). Moreover, MLEvolve also outperforms specialized algorithm discovery methods including AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm optimization tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [Central Description Length (CDL) Clustering Validation Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05230)
*Mahdi Shamsi,Soosan Beheshti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的聚类验证指标——中央描述长度（CDL），该指标通过计算不可观测的真实聚类中心描述长度的概率上界，仅利用可观测数据（簇内紧致度、估计中心和协方差）来评估聚类质量，无需标签。在非凸、任意形状的合成数据及图像基准测试中，CDL比传统CVI更准确地选择了参考聚类数并获得了更高的调整兰德指数（ARI），且无需额外的核预处理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚类验证指标（CVI）基于欧氏紧致度和分离度构建，偏向紧致凸形分区，在处理非凸、不规则或变密度的数据时性能下降，而替代方法（如核变换）又需额外调优和计算成本。因此需要一种无需标签、无需核预处理且能有效处理任意形状聚类的验证指标。

Method: 利用观测到的簇内紧致度、估计的聚类中心和协方差矩阵，计算真实聚类中心描述长度的概率上界。该上界将簇内紧致度与中心位移融合为一个可计算的量，仅依赖数据、分区、估计中心和协方差等可观测信息，无需真实标签。

Result: 在非凸和任意形状的合成基准上，CDL-CVI比传统CVI更频繁地选出正确的参考聚类数，并达到更高的ARI值，且无需核预处理。在图像基准（MNIST、CIFAR-10、STL-10）上，使用冻结的无监督嵌入，CDL-CVI在K-means、DBSCAN和谱聚类上均返回接近真实类别数的聚类数。

Conclusion: CDL-CVI是一种有效的无监督聚类验证指标，能够在不依赖标签和额外核预处理的情况下，对非凸、任意形状的数据进行可靠的聚类评估，在合成和真实图像数据上均优于传统CVI。

Abstract: Selecting a clustering algorithm and its hyperparameters without labels is a common difficulty in engineering machine learning pipelines that work with unsupervised analysis of sensor, image, or process data. Clustering validation indices (CVIs) provide internal scores for ranking candidate clusterings, but most popular CVIs are built from Euclidean compactness and separation terms and so tend to favour compact, convex partitions. Their performance is known to degrade on non convex, irregular, or variable density data, where kernel transformations or alternative distance measures are typically used at the cost of additional tuning and computation. This paper introduces the Central Description Length (CDL) clustering validation index. CDL uses the observed within cluster compactness, the estimated cluster centers, and the estimated cluster covariances to compute a probabilistic upper bound on the description length associated with the unobservable true cluster centers. The bound condenses intra cluster compactness and centroid displacement into a single computable quantity and is evaluated on the partition produced by any clustering algorithm. The implementation uses only observable quantities (the data, the partition, the estimated centers, and the estimated covariances) and does not use ground truth labels. On synthetic benchmarks with non convex and arbitrary shape clusters, CDL-CVI selected the reference number of clusters more often and reached higher Adjusted Rand Index (ARI) values than the conventional CVIs we tested, without an additional kernel preprocessing stage. On image benchmarks (MNIST, CIFAR-10, STL-10) clustered from frozen unsupervised embeddings, CDL-CVI returned cluster numbers close to the reference class counts across K-means, DBSCAN, and spectral clustering in the reported trials.

</details>


### [235] [HyFAD: Hybrid Time-Frequency Diffusion with Frequency-Aware Embedding for Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05239)
*Hongfan Gao,Wangmeng Shen,Bin Yang,Jilin Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: HyFAD是一种混合时频扩散模型，通过耦合时频域的双重去噪过程实现从粗到细的时间序列填补，并引入频率感知步长嵌入来提升高频重构精度，在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在时间序列建模中存在三个关键问题：频率敏感的去噪能力不足、高频成分重建困难、全局趋势与局部动态的平衡难以兼顾。

Method: 基于DDPM范式，HyFAD构建了耦合时频扩散框架：时间域扩散过程先捕捉低频全局趋势，频率域扩散过程再细化高频谱分量；并引入频率感知步长嵌入，利用扩散步与频谱分量的关系提供逐步的频谱引导，实现更精确的波段重建。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，HyFAD达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: HyFAD通过耦合时频域的双重扩散机制和频率感知步长嵌入，有效解决了现有方法在时间序列填补中的频率敏感去噪和高频重构难题。

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated strong performance in time series modeling due to their ability to progressively capture complex data distributions through iterative denoising. However, existing approaches struggle with frequency-sensitive denoising, high-frequency reconstruction and balancing global trends with local dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HyFAD}, a \textbf{Hy}brid time-frequency \textbf{D}iffusion model with \textbf{F}requency-\textbf{A}ware embedding for time series imputation. Built upon the DDPM paradigm, HyFAD adopts a coupled time-frequency diffusion framework, in which the reverse denoising proceeds sequentially from the time domain to the frequency domain, enabling coarse-to-fine generation. Specifically, the time-domain diffusion process captures low-frequency global trends, while the frequency-domain diffusion process refines high-frequency spectral components. We further introduce a frequency-aware step embedding that exploits the relationship between diffusion steps and spectral components, providing step-dependent spectral guidance and facilitates more accurate band-wise reconstruction. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that HyFAD achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our source code is available at https://github.com/hongfangao/HyFAD.

</details>


### [236] [Deterministic Envelopes for Tamed SGLD: Decoupling Stochastic-Gradient Noise and Localizing Taming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05242)
*Yiwei Zhou,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构保持的框架来设计随机梯度Langevin算法中的有界分母，通过使用确定性包络代替依赖于相同随机梯度实现的分母，避免因梯度依赖分母产生的稳态偏差，并采用软硬混合包络策略平衡偏差与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机梯度Langevin算法中常用有界分母来稳定非全局Lipschitz的漂移项，但当分母依赖于与分子相同的随机梯度实现时，会改变随机预言机本身，即使原始随机梯度无偏，也会引入稳态偏差。

Method: 提出结构保持框架：在采样预言机噪声之前固定分母，使用局部确定性包络，避免在典型区域进行不必要的有界处理。在该框架内，进一步提出混合包络设计——典型区域使用软包络，稀有极端区域使用硬尾控制。

Result: 理论分析了稳态误差如何分解为预言机依赖的有界化偏差和确定性稳定化引入的剩余误差，识别了远尾条件以解释局部软包络的局限性。实验验证了随机分母的稳态失真、确定性包络设计的偏差降低以及混合构造的稳定效果。

Conclusion: 确定性包络设计能有效避免随机分母引入的偏差，混合软硬包络策略在保持稳定性的同时进一步降低偏差，为随机梯度Langevin算法的稳定化提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Stochastic-gradient Langevin algorithms often use tamed denominators to stabilize non-globally Lipschitz drifts. This paper shows that when the denominator depends on the same stochastic-gradient realization as the numerator, the taming step changes the stochastic oracle itself and can create a stationary bias even if the original stochastic gradient is unbiased. We propose a structure-preserving framework for designing tamed denominators. It fixes the denominator before the oracle noise is sampled and uses localized deterministic envelopes to avoid unnecessary taming in typical regions. These kernels keep the stabilizing effect of taming while avoiding the bias introduced by a gradient-dependent denominator. Our theory explains how the stationary error splits into the bias caused by oracle-dependent taming and the remaining error introduced by deterministic stabilization. Within this deterministic-envelope family, the analysis identifies a far-tail condition that explains the limitation of local soft envelopes and motivates a hybrid member: soft in the typical region, but protected by hard-tail control on rare excursions. Experiments confirm the predicted stationary distortions of random denominators, the bias reduction of deterministic-envelope designs, and the stabilizing effect of the hybrid construction.

</details>


### [237] [Harnessing Source Heterogeneity for Cluster-Structured Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05258)
*Xiaohui Yin,Jun Jin,Shane J. Sacco,Robert H. Aseltine,Kun Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Trans-GLMC方法，用于处理迁移学习中源群体异质性问题。该方法通过聚类结构识别具有不同可迁移性的源子群，在广义线性模型框架下实现从多个辅助源向目标群体的自适应迁移，并在自杀风险预测研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标群体数据有限但存在多个异质性辅助源时，现有迁移学习方法将源选择简化为二元（有用/无用）决策，忽略了源群体间可能存在的结构化差异（如聚类模式），导致信息利用不充分。

Method: Trans-GLMC方法包含三个步骤：1) 基于系数距离构建目标与候选源之间的距离矩阵，恢复潜在源聚类结构；2) 结合全局融合、聚类内精炼和目标去偏，产生适应检测到的结构的估计量；3) 建立非渐近误差界，在存在有意义目标聚类时优于非聚类方法。

Result: 在模拟实验和CHIME自杀风险研究（636,758名患者，27家医院）中，Trans-GLMC改进了设施级别的预测性能，识别出具有可解释性的医院社区结构，并恢复了临床一致的自杀风险因素。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了源群体异质性下的结构化迁移学习问题，通过聚类策略替代二元选择，在理论上保证了误差上界不会退化，并在实际数据中展现了更好的预测性能和可解释性。

Abstract: Transfer learning is a natural strategy when a target population has limited data but multiple related auxiliary sources are available. A central difficulty is source heterogeneity: auxiliary sources may not be equally useful, and their usefulness may vary in a structured, cluster-like fashion. Existing transfer-learning methods often reduce source selection to a binary informative/non-informative decision, overlooking subgroups of sources with differential transferability. Motivated by a suicide-risk study using data from the Connecticut Hospital Information Management Exchange (CHIME), comprising 636,758 patients across 27 hospitals, we propose Trans-GLMC, a cluster-structured transfer-learning procedure for generalized linear models. The CHIME setting illustrates the core challenge: hospital-specific risk models are unstable because suicide attempts are rare at any single facility, whereas indiscriminate pooling across hospitals can obscure facility-level differences in patient mix and risk profiles. Trans-GLMC first constructs a coefficient-based distance among the target and candidate sources to recover latent source clusters. It then combines global fusion, within-cluster refinement, and target debiasing to produce an estimator that adapts to the detected structure. We establish a non-asymptotic error bound that improves over its unclustered counterpart whenever a meaningful target cluster exists and matches the unclustered rate up to constants otherwise. In simulations and in the CHIME study, Trans-GLMC improves facility-specific prediction, identifies interpretable communities of hospitals with mutual transferability, and recovers clinically coherent suicide-risk factors.

</details>


### [238] [TabSODA: Tabular Diffusion based Imputation with Skip Pattern Detection and Ordinal Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05361)
*Yuyu Chen,Taehyo Kim,Hai Shu,Yang Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TabSODA是一种基于扩散模型的大规模调查缺失数据插补方法，通过EM框架处理结构性跳过项和有序变量，在PATH和NSDUH数据集上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格扩散方法在大规模调查数据插补中面临两个未解决挑战：(1) 问卷设计导致的结构性跳过项（不应被插补）常与项目无响应混为一谈；(2) 有序响应的序数编码被忽略，通常采用独热或模拟位编码将其视为名义变量。

Method: 基于Elucidated Diffusion Model (EDM)框架，采用EM（期望最大化）进行扩散插补。通过去噪损失和反向时间采样器传播结构性跳过；对有序变量使用累积概率标量潜变量表示，名义变量保留模拟位编码。当有码本跳过掩码时直接使用，否则通过CART的跳过模式挖掘器（TabSODA+SKIP变体）从原始响应和问卷顺序中估计掩码。

Result: 在PATH和NSDUH两个美国全国代表性调查中，TabSODA在MCAR、MAR、MNAR掩码下，有序变量MACE降低高达23.7%，分类准确率提升高达9%。跳过模式挖掘器在两个数据集上达到近完美精确度，TabSODA+SKIP接近码本掩码变体的性能。

Conclusion: TabSODA有效解决了大规模调查数据中结构性跳过和有序变量处理两大难题，为调查数据插补提供了更精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Missing data imputation in large-scale surveys faces two challenges that are not well handled by current tabular diffusion methods. First, \emph{structural skips}, cells made inapplicable by questionnaire design, should not be imputed but are often conflated with item nonresponse. Second, \emph{ordinal} responses encode ordered categories, yet most pipelines treat them as nominal levels through one-hot or analog-bit encodings. We introduce \textbf{TabSODA} (\textbf{Tab}ular diffusion with \textbf{S}kip pattern detection and \textbf{O}r\textbf{d}inal \textbf{A}wareness), an Expectation-Maximization (EM)-based diffusion imputer built on the Elucidated Diffusion Model (EDM) framework. TabSODA propagates structural skips through the denoising loss and reverse-time sampler, and represents ordinal variables with cumulative-probit scalar latents while retaining analog-bit encodings for nominal variables. When a codebook skip mask is available, TabSODA uses it directly; otherwise, the TabSODA+SKIP variant estimates the mask from raw responses and questionnaire order using a CART-based skip-pattern miner. On Population Assessment of Tobacco and Health (PATH) study and the National Survey on Drug Use and Health (NSDUH), two nationally representative U.S.\ surveys, TabSODA reduces ordinal MACE by up to $23.7\%$ and improves categorical accuracy by up to $9\%$ over the strongest baseline across MCAR, MAR, and MNAR masking. The skip miner achieves near-perfect precision on both datasets, allowing TabSODA+SKIP to closely track the codebook-mask variant.

</details>


### [239] [Environment-Robust Representation Learning with Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05365)
*Yuli Slavutsky,Matthew Shen,Bohan Wu,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多环境预测的贝叶斯方法，假设环境改变潜在变量的分布，而观测变量和目标的生成机制在给定潜在变量条件下保持稳定。通过变分推断和经验贝叶斯方法，模型能够跨环境进行预测，在天文学、微生物组和ICU脓毒症预测等任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多环境预测中，环境（如医院、临床队列）会改变潜在变量的分布（如患者状态的患病率），但状态与观测数据及结果之间的关系保持稳定。现有方法未充分利用这种结构，因此需要一种能够显式建模这种不变性并利用多环境数据进行迁移预测的方法。

Method: 1) 构建贝叶斯模型，假设环境仅改变潜在变量的分布，而条件生成机制不变；2) 推导变分目标函数，分解为每个环境的项和一个跨环境平衡项；3) 使用经验贝叶斯方法设置先验并融入目标函数；4) 提出摊销变分算法进行后验近似，利用学习到的潜在变量在新环境中进行预测。

Result: 在模拟实验以及天文学源识别、微生物组疾病检测、ICU脓毒症预测三个真实场景中，该方法在新环境下的预测性能均优于先前的方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模环境间潜在分布的差异并保持条件生成机制不变，结合变分推断和经验贝叶斯，能够有效提升多环境预测问题在新环境下的泛化能力。

Abstract: We consider multi-environment prediction problems. We assume the environments change the distribution of a latent variable, while the mechanisms generating observed covariates and targets remain stable conditional on that variable. For example, hospitals or clinical cohorts may differ in the prevalence of latent patient states, even though the relationships between those states, physiological measurements, and outcomes remain unchanged. Given a dataset from multiple environments, we formulate a Bayesian model for such problems and derive the corresponding variational objective. We show that this objective decomposes into per-environment terms and an additional cross-environment balancing term induced by the model's structure. We use an empirical Bayes method to set the prior and incorporate it into the objective. Based on this objective, we develop an amortized variational algorithm for posterior approximation, and use the resulting learned latent variables to form predictions in new environments.We study our approach through simulations and real-world studies of astronomical source identification, microbiome-based disease detection, and ICU sepsis prediction. Across these settings, our method outperforms previous approaches for prediction in new environments.

</details>


### [240] [Sparse Functional Singular Value Decomposition for Biclustering and Triclustering Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05488)
*Yue Zhao,Thierry Chekouo,Sandra Safo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Tri-SfSVD框架，一种统一稀疏函数奇异值分解方法，用于在纵向高维稀疏不规则观测数据中发现双聚类和三聚类结构，无需插值或同质性假设。模拟实验证明其优于现有方法。应用于IBD多组学数据识别到三个双聚类，揭示样本-微生物通路的关联；应用于多通道EEG数据识别到三个三聚类，揭示样本-时空脑活动模式的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂疾病（如IBD）亚型识别需要捕获纵向组学数据中的潜在模式，但现有（双）聚类和函数数据分析方法难以处理高维、稀疏采样、不规则观测的纵向数据；现有函数双聚类方法依赖临时插值或施加严格的形状同质性假设，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出Tri-SfSVD框架，将连续轨迹估计与受试者、特征和时间维度的同步选择整合到统一的稀疏函数奇异值分解优化框架中，通过在受试者、变量和时间子区域施加稀疏惩罚，直接在观测数据上工作，发现受试者层面、受试者-特征层面和受试者-特征-时间层面的局部结构。

Result: 模拟实验证明Tri-SfSVD在高维设置下优于现有方法。在IBD多组学数据中识别出三个双聚类，将样本簇与IBD相关临床特征及特定细菌类群相关的微生物通路群联系起来。在多通道EEG数据中识别出三个三聚类，将样本簇与酒精相关表型及局部脑活动模式联系起来，包括同一空间区域内由时间子区域区分的亚组差异。

Conclusion: Tri-SfSVD是一种有效的统一框架，能够在纵向高维稀疏不规则数据中同时实现双聚类和三聚类，为疾病异质性提供可解释的受试者-通路/时空模式关联。

Abstract: Identifying subtypes of complex conditions, such as Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD), often requires capturing latent patterns in longitudinal omics data. However, these data are typically high-dimensional, sparsely sampled, and irregularly observed over time, posing substantial challenges for conventional (bi)clustering and functional data analysis methods. We propose Tri-SfSVD, a unified sparse functional Singular Value Decomposition framework for discovering biclusters and triclusters in longitudinal data. Unlike existing functional biclustering methods that rely on ad hoc imputation or enforce restrictive shape-homogeneity assumptions, Tri-SfSVD integrates continuous trajectory estimation with simultaneous subject, feature, and temporal selection within a single optimization framework. By imposing sparse penalties across subjects, variables, and temporal subregions, the proposed method works directly on observed data to uncover localized structures at the subject, subject-feature, and subject-feature-time levels. Extensive simulations demonstrate that Tri-SfSVD outperforms existing approaches in high-dimensional settings. Applied to IBD multi-omics data, the method identified three biclusters linking sample clusters with distinct IBD-related clinical characteristics to microbial pathway groups associated with specific bacterial taxa, providing interpretable subject-pathway associations for characterizing disease heterogeneity. Applied to multi-channel EEG data, the method identified three triclusters linking sample clusters with distinct alcohol-related phenotypes to localized brain activity patterns, including subgroup differences separated by temporal subregions within the same spatial region.

</details>


### [241] [Conformal Risk-Averse Decision Making with Action Conditional Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05551)
*Zihan Zhu,Shayan Kiyani,George Pappas. Hamed Hassani*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了动作条件共形预测（action-conditional conformal prediction），通过将预测集的条件保证从边际层面扩展到每个具体动作，实现了在有限样本下对条件风险价值（CVaR）的最优风险厌恶决策，并基于pinball损失最小化提出了高效算法。实验证明该方法在动作条件性能上显著优于共形基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形预测方法仅提供边际安全保证，无法满足风险厌恶决策者对每个具体动作的安全性要求（如高风险场景中不同动作的风险差异巨大）。作者希望将安全保证从边际级别细化到动作条件级别，以支持更精细的风险控制。

Method: (i) 提出动作条件共形预测，使预测集的安全保证显式地依赖于决策者所采取的每个动作；(ii) 证明动作条件预测集可作为风险厌恶决策者优化动作条件条件风险价值（CVaR）时的可行决策空间代理；(iii) 基于pinball损失最小化提出有限样本算法，将Gibbs等人(2025)的框架与动作条件保证相连接。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在动作条件性能上显著优于共形基线方法。

Conclusion: 动作条件共形预测将安全保证从边际层面细化到每个动作层面，为风险敏感应用（如自动驾驶、医疗决策）提供了更强、更细粒度的理论保障，且算法实现高效。

Abstract: Reliable decision making pipelines powered by machine learning models require uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods that come with explicit safety guarantees. Conformal prediction provides such UQ by wrapping ML predictions into prediction sets, and recent work by Kiyani et al. (2025b) established that these sets can be translated into optimal risk-averse decision policies -- yet only inheriting marginal safety guarantees. We generalize and strengthen their results by (i) introducing action-conditional conformal prediction, which yields safety guarantees conditioned explicitly on each action taken by the decision maker, (ii) showing that action-conditional prediction sets serve as a proxy for the feasible decision space for risk-averse decision makers aiming to optimize action-conditional value-at-risk, and (iii) proposing a principled finite-sample algorithm based on pinball-loss minimization, connecting the framework of Gibbs et al. (2025) to action-conditional guarantees. Experiments on two real-world datasets confirm that our approach significantly improves action-conditional performance over conformal baselines.

</details>


### [242] [Finding Most Influential Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05919)
*Lucas D. Konrad,Nikolas Kuschnig*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将最具影响力子集（MIS）选择问题——即寻找移除后最大程度改变目标估计量的size-k子集——从指数级搜索复杂度降低为O(n)复杂度的一参数序列top-k问题。通过Dinkelbach算法实现有限步终止，并证明了在固定残差化输入下的全局最优性，以及含估计 nuisance 函数时的近似保证和精确集恢复条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MIS选择需要搜索C(n,k)个子集，计算上不可行，亟需高效算法。

Method: 将带线性分式留出集效应的估计量的MIS选择转化为单参数序列的top-k问题，利用Dinkelbach方法迭代求解，每轮迭代复杂度O(n)，有限步终止。

Result: 对固定残差化输入可返回全局最优集；含估计 nuisance 函数时，均匀分母与生成分数稳定性可近似一阶正交分数目标，在分离条件下实现精确集恢复。

Conclusion: 该方法能恢复此前计算上无法获取的精确MIS，在模拟和应用中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Identifying most influential sets (MIS) - size-$k$ subsets whose removal maximally changes a target estimand - is typically infeasible because it requires searching over $\binom{n}{k}$ subsets. For estimands with linear-fractional leave-set-out effects, we show that MIS selection reduces to a one-parameter sequence of top-$k$ problems. Dinkelbach's method yields an algorithm with $\mathcal{O}(n)$ cost per iteration and finite termination. For fixed residualized inputs, the algorithm returns a globally optimal set for the univariate ratio objective, including the oracle-residualized partial linear model. With estimated nuisance functions, uniform denominator and generated-score stability imply approximation to the first-order oracle orthogonal-score objective; exact set recovery follows under a separation condition. Simulations and applications show that the method recovers exact MIS that were previously computationally inaccessible.

</details>


### [243] [EML-CD: Causal Mechanism Recovery via EML Symbolic Trees in Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05942)
*Sota Asanuma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出EML-CD框架，将可组合初等函数的EML算子融入因果结构学习，使每个边机制表示为门控EML二叉树并自动发现闭式因果方程，在多个基准上实现可解释的因果机制恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的非线性因果发现方法虽能恢复DAG结构，但每个因果机制仍是黑箱；且从NN权重中提取因果机制是ill-posed的。亟需一种既能学习因果结构又能提供可解释因果机制的方法。

Method: 将EML算子（可从单个二元算子组合初等函数）集成到因果结构学习中，每条边机制表示为门控EML二叉树，自动发现闭式因果方程，可直接计算解析雅可比矩阵。

Result: Sachs蛋白信号数据上SHD=11.2±0.4，与PC/GES相当、优于CAM；边检测精确率0.756、召回率0.365。双变量测试恢复11个初等函数族中的10个。符号合成基准上f-MSE远低于固定SINDy字典（3.67 vs 7644）。Causal Chambers数据集上深度2模型F1优于线性OLS-BIC（0.444 vs 0.273）。

Conclusion: EML-CD有效弥合了因果结构学习与机制可解释性之间的鸿沟，在保持竞争性结构恢复性能的同时提供可解释的闭式因果方程，为可解释因果发现提供了新方向。

Abstract: Neural network (NN)-based nonlinear causal discovery methods recover DAG structure but leave each causal mechanism as a black box. Waxman et al. argued that extracting causal mechanisms from NN weights is ill-posed. We propose EML-CD, a framework that integrates the EML operator (capable of composing elementary functions from a single binary operator) into causal structure learning, with interpretable mechanism recovery as the primary objective. EML-CD represents each edge mechanism as a gated EML binary tree and automatically discovers closed-form causal equations. Analytical Jacobians can be directly computed from the output equations, enabling quantitative understanding of causal effects. On real data (Sachs protein signaling, d=11), EML-CD achieves SHD=11.2 +/- 0.4 (5-seed mean; baselines are single deterministic runs), on par with PC/GES within seed variance and below CAM, while attaching closed-form equations to each detected edge (precision 0.756, recall 0.365). In a controlled bivariate test with known mechanisms, EML-CD recovers 10 of 11 elementary function families faithfully (held-out shape correlation >= 0.96; only high-frequency sine is partial). On a symbolic synthetic benchmark, EML-CD attains a substantially lower and more stable held-out mechanism f-MSE than a fixed SINDy dictionary (mean 3.67 vs. 7644, the latter inflated by catastrophic extrapolation on one seed), although its structure recovery (SHD 14.0) only matches the dictionary and stays below specialized optimizers; on the Causal Chambers light-tunnel subset, a depth-2 model improves F1 over linear OLS-BIC (0.444 vs. 0.273).

</details>


### [244] [Fast and Robust Convergence Rate for TD(0) with Linear Function Approximation, Universal Learning Steps and I.I.D. Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05967)
*Ziad Kobeissi,Éloïse Berthier*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了带线性函数逼近的TD(0)算法在有限时间内的收敛性，建立了均方误差的O(1/k)收敛率，该速率具有最优的迭代依赖、对病态问题的鲁棒性以及小于11的乘法常数，并提出了改进变体PCTD(0)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TD(0)的O(1/k)收敛率依赖于线性参数化非中心协方差矩阵的最小特征值，导致在病态条件下收敛缓慢，亟需建立不依赖该特征值的鲁棒收敛界。

Method: 针对on-policy i.i.d.样本，采用常数步长和Polyak-Juditsky平均方法，建立TD(0)的MSE收敛率；进一步提出PCTD(0)变体，在强混合马尔可夫链假设下获得更优收敛性质。

Result: 建立了O(1/k)收敛率，该速率：(i)对迭代次数k具有最优依赖；(ii)仅依赖初始误差和模型无关常数，对病态问题鲁棒；(iii)乘法常数界小于11。PCTD(0)在强混合假设下具有更好的收敛性。

Conclusion: TD(0)的收敛率摆脱了对最小特征值的依赖，显著优于现有结果；PCTD(0)进一步拓展了应用场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the finite-time behavior of the TD(0) temporal-difference method with linear function approximation (LFA). We consider on-policy independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) samples, a constant learning step, and the Polyak-Juditsky averaging method. We establish a new convergence rate, for the Mean-Square Error (MSE) on the approximated function, that is (i) fast in the sense that it admits an optimal dependency in the number of iterations k (i.e., of order 1/k), (ii) robust to ill-conditioning: it only depends on an initial error and modelindependent constants and (iii) sharp up to a multiplicative constant lower than 11. In particular, it does not depend on the smallest eigenvalue of the uncentered covariance matrix of the linear parametrization, unlike all pre-existing O(1/k) rates in the TD(0) literature. We also introduce PCTD(0), a variant of TD(0), which benefits from better convergence properties under an additional assumption of strong mixing on the Markov Chain.

</details>


### [245] [Adaptive Learning Rates with Surrogate Probability for Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06043)
*Jongyeong Lee,Junya Honda,Shinji Ito,Chansoo Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于替代概率函数的自适应学习率方法，使FTPL算法在保持计算简单性的同时，首次实现任意形状参数α>1的Pareto扰动下最优双世界（BOBW）保证，并推广到专家建议的赌博机问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FTRL框架中自适应学习率已取得良好BOBW保证，但其计算高效替代方案FTPL由于无优化特性，难以设计依赖概率的自适应学习率，导致其BOBW保证相对有限。

Method: 引入替代概率函数，仅利用可用量（无需精确概率）计算自适应学习率；基于该学习率，对任意形状参数α>1的Pareto扰动FTPL提供BOBW保证，并推广到专家建议赌博机问题。

Result: 所提方法在保持FTPL计算简单性的同时，实现了概率依赖的自适应能力；在Pareto扰动（α>1）下取得BOBW保证，推广了先前仅适用于α=2的结论；并在专家建议赌博机问题中也取得BOBW保证。

Conclusion: 提出的替代概率方法论不仅适用于FTPL和学习率设计，还可能对其他算法框架具有独立价值，实现了计算效率与理论最优性的统一。

Abstract: Follow-the-regularized-leader framework has shown effectiveness and flexibility in online learning problems, where the choice of learning rates are known to be crucial. Recently, adaptive learning rates defined in terms of the arm-selection probabilities, obtained by solving convex optimization, have achieved improved best-of-both-worlds (BOBW) guarantees in various bandit problems. In contrast, BOBW guarantees for its computationally efficient alternative, follow-the-perturbed-leader (FTPL), remain relatively limited since its optimization-free nature ironically makes the design of adaptive, probability-dependent learning rates non-trivial. To address this challenge, we propose an adaptive learning rate for FTPL by introducing surrogate probability functions that can be computed only from the available quantities, without requiring the exact probabilities. Based on these learning rates with surrogate functions, we provide the BOBW guarantee for FTPL with Pareto perturbations for any shape parameter $α>1$, generalizing prior results restricted to specific choices of $α=2$. We further show the BOBW guarantees for FTPL with adaptive learning rates in the bandit problem with expert advices. Our approach preserves the computational simplicity of FTPL while enabling probability-dependent adaptivity, and the surrogate-based methodology may be of independent interest in other algorithmic frameworks beyond FTPL and learning rate designs.

</details>


### [246] [Effective Dimensionality as an Operator Invariant for Physics-Preserving Constraint Adaptation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06171)
*Cornelius Otchere,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用Fisher信息矩阵量化物理约束模型的有效自由度(deff)，证明对于有限维核的微分算子，deff收敛于核维度，成为连续算子的结构不变量；并据此提出子空间投影策略，在不重新训练的情况下高效适应新边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) 因共享参数空间同时满足控制微分方程和边界条件而存在任务干扰问题，需要量化并解决这种结构性冲突。

Method: 1) 利用Fisher信息矩阵定义有效自由度(deff)，衡量参数方向中未被微分算子约束的维度；2) 对有限维核算子证明deff收敛于核维度，对无限维核算子则衡量网络的有限维表示带宽；3) 提出子空间投影策略，将参数更新投影到预训练物理算子的零空间中，在不干扰已学物理规律的前提下适应新边界条件。

Result: deff对于有限维核算子精确收敛于核维度，与网络宽度、深度或激活函数无关；子空间投影策略在数秒至数分钟内实现与新边界条件匹配，质量与梯度微调相当，但耗时显著更少。

Conclusion: deff可作为先验结构诊断工具，将良定问题的deff驱动至零可确保物理和边界约束吸收了网络的自由方向；子空间投影策略为边界适应提供了高效替代方案。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks inherently suffer from task interference because they rely on a shared parameter space to satisfy both governing differential equations and boundary conditions. We analyze this structural conflict using the Fisher Information Matrix to quantify the effective degrees of freedom ($d_{eff}$) in a physics-constrained model. Unlike the classical $d_{eff}$ which measures how many parameter directions are informed by data against a statistical prior, our $d_{eff}$ measures the dimension of the parameter directions unconstrained by the differential operator. For operators with finite-dimensional kernel, we show that $d_{eff}$ converges to the kernel dimension exactly, independent of network width, depth, or activation function, recasting it from a fit diagnostic into a structural invariant of the underlying continuous operator. For operators with infinite-dimensional kernel, $d_{eff}$ instead measures the network's finite-dimensional representational bandwidth for that kernel rather than recovering an integer invariant. Importantly, $d_{eff}$ also serves as an a priori structural diagnostic. Driving $d_{eff}$ of a well-posed problem to zero certifies that the physics and boundary constraints have absorbed the network's free directions. Building on this characterization, we introduce subspace projection strategies for boundary adaptation. Rather than retraining from scratch, we project parameter updates into the null space of the pre-trained physics operator so that new boundary conditions are satisfied without disturbing the learned physics. Gradient-based fine-tuning can match or exceed this but needs more wall-clock time and tuning, whereas subspace projection delivers near-equivalent quality in seconds to minutes. We validate on linear and nonlinear operators, demonstrating accurate adaptation to initial and boundary shifts and unencountered constraint types.

</details>


### [247] [Diffusion Models Observe Only Gradients: A Geometric Perspective on Score Matching Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06179)
*Naïl B. Khelifa,Richard E. Turner,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明L²分数匹配误差并非衡量扩散模型边际分布质量的正确指标，通过Helmholtz-Hodge分解识别出只有梯度分量影响边际Fokker-Planck动力学，并给出了理论下界、KL散度上界以及可计算的梯度分量估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型训练使用L²分数匹配误差，但该指标与采样质量之间的理论联系可能并非最优，需要更精确的度量来反映边际分布的真实差异。

Method: 利用Helmholtz-Hodge分解将分数误差分解为梯度分量和螺线管分量，分析两者对边际Fokker-Planck动力学的不同影响；通过反例证明L²误差无法一致下界化分布散度；推导仅依赖于梯度分量的KL散度上界；提出基于对偶Sobolev恒等式的梯度分量估计器。

Result: (1) 不存在L²误差的单调函数能均匀下界化任何分布散度（不可能性结果）；(2) 得到了仅依赖梯度分量的KL散度上界，比标准Girsanov界更紧；(3) 梯度分量估计器与样本质量的相关性显著优于完整L²误差。

Conclusion: L²分数误差不是衡量扩散模型边际分布质量的正确内在指标，梯度分量才是与采样质量更相关的度量，为扩散模型的理论分析和评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models are typically trained by minimizing the $L^2$ score matching error, and standard theoretical analyses rely on this quantity to bound the sampling discrepancy between the learned and target distributions. We show the $L^2$ score error is not the right intrinsic measure of marginal distributional quality: a learned diffusion model can incur arbitrarily large $L^2$ score error while perfectly matching the target distribution. By decomposing score errors into a gradient and a solenoidal component (a Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition), we identify the geometric reason behind this: only the gradient component enters the marginal Fokker-Planck dynamics, while the solenoidal component is structurally invisible. We make this precise in three results. First, building on the corrected geometry, we prove an impossibility result: no monotone function of the $L^2$ score error can uniformly lower bound any divergence between the learned and target distributions. Second, we derive an upper bound on the Kullback-Leibler divergence that depends only on the observable gradient component of the error, tightening the standard Girsanov bound and identifying its looseness as the cost of operating on path-space rather than marginal-space dynamics. Third, we give a tractable estimator of the gradient component via a dual Sobolev identity, which is shown to empirically correlate substantially better with sample quality than the full $L^2$ error.

</details>


### [248] [Anchor PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06233)
*Benedikt Seiter,Anya Fries,Julius von Kügelgen,Jonas Peters*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Anchor PCA方法，用于多领域数据的鲁棒无监督降维，通过对修改后的目标矩阵进行PCA，在解释总体方差与领域间低秩嵌入一致性之间取得平衡，恢复最大不变子空间，并在模拟和真实气体传感器数据上证明其优于池化基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PCA用于多领域数据时，对合并数据做PCA可能聚焦于仅在少数领域呈现高方差的虚假方向，无法找到在未见过的相似领域仍能解释大部分方差的鲁棒嵌入。本文旨在找到一种能捕捉共享变异方向的多领域鲁棒降维方法。

Method: 提出Anchor PCA方法，通过优化一个权衡总体解释方差与共享嵌入和领域特定低秩嵌入之间一致性的目标函数，将问题转化为对修改后的目标矩阵进行PCA，从而可高效求解。理论上证明了Anchor PCA能恢复最大不变子空间，并具有在领域特定协方差膨胀受限下的极小极大重构解释。

Result: 在模拟数据和具有时间漂移的真实气体传感器数据上，Anchor PCA能够恢复最大不变子空间，并且在未见过的领域上比池化基线和最坏情况替代方法解释更多方差。

Conclusion: Anchor PCA是一种有前景的多领域数据鲁棒无监督降维方法，能够在领域间共享变异方向上获得鲁棒的嵌入表示。

Abstract: Principal component analysis (PCA) is one of the most widely used unsupervised dimension reduction techniques. We study PCA for data from multiple related domains. Since principal components generally differ across domains, one way to obtain a shared low-rank embedding is to perform PCA on the pooled data. However, this approach can focus on spurious directions that exhibit high variation in only a few domains. To find a robust embedding that still explains most variance in unseen but similar domains, we propose instead to focus on shared directions of variation. To this end, we introduce Anchor PCA which trades off overall explained variance with agreement between the shared and domain-specific low-rank embeddings. Anchor PCA amounts to PCA on a modified target matrix and thus can be solved efficiently. Moreover, we show that Anchor PCA recovers a maximal invariant subspace and admits a minimax reconstruction interpretation under bounded domain-specific covariance inflations. On simulated and real-world gas sensor data with temporal drift, we demonstrate, respectively, that Anchor PCA recovers the maximally invariant subspace and yields embeddings that explain more variance on unseen domains than the pooling baseline and a worst-case alternative. Taken together, these findings establish Anchor PCA as a promising approach to robust unsupervised dimension reduction from multi-domain data.

</details>


### [249] [Discrete Causal Representations from Heterogeneous Domains: A Bayesian Approach with Social Survey Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06288)
*Ankur Garg,Michael Stettler,Aaron Schein,Julius von Kügelgen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一个贝叶斯方法，用于从多环境数据中学习离散因果概念的因果表示，使用序贯蒙特卡洛采样近似多模态后验，并在社会调查数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表示学习旨在推断产生观测数据的潜在因果概念，但现有工作缺乏不确定性感知的方法和实际应用案例。多环境数据中的分布偏移通常源于底层因果机制的稀疏局部变化，需要能够建模这种结构的表示学习方法。

Method: 采用贝叶斯层次模型，将因果假设和可解释性需求编码为先验和参数选择；针对离散因果概念和多节点软干预场景，利用序贯蒙特卡洛采样近似多模态后验分布。

Result: 在社会调查数据（文化价值观、政治观点作为潜在概念，调查回答作为观测，国家/州作为环境）上，模型推断出有意义的高层概念及其因果关系。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯因果表示学习方法能有效处理多环境异质数据，为复杂现实数据提供不确定性感知的因果表示学习框架。

Abstract: Causal representation learning aims to infer the high-level latent causal concepts that give rise to observed low-level measurements. This is particularly relevant for heterogeneous data from different environments or domains since distribution shifts often arise through sparse, localized changes in some of the underlying causal mechanisms, while other parts of the generative process remain unchanged. Whereas identifiability of causal representations has been studied extensively, practical uncertainty-aware methods and real-world use cases remain less explored. In this work, we propose a Bayesian approach to learning causal representations from multi-environment data, focusing on the case of discrete causal concepts and unknown multi-node soft interventions. To this end, we translate causal assumptions and interpretability desiderata into suitable priors and parametric choices within a hierarchical model. We then devise an inference scheme based on sequential Monte Carlo sampling to approximate the resulting multimodal posterior. We showcase our approach through case studies on social survey data, where latent causal concepts correspond to cultural values or political opinions, measurements to survey responses, and environments to different countries or states. Our model infers meaningful high-level concepts and plausible causal relations among them, demonstrating its utility for learning causal representations of complex real-world data.

</details>


### [250] [Symmetric Divergence and Normalized Similarity: A Unified Topological Framework for Representation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06342)
*Yan Wang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了统一的拓扑数据分析工具包，包含对称表示拓扑散度（SRTD）及其高效变体SRTD-lite，以及归一化拓扑相似度（NTS），用于神经表征的细粒度结构诊断和标准化评估。SRTD解决了原RTD框架的启发式不对称问题，NTS通过归一化实现了在[-1,1]范围内的尺度不变度量，克服了样本依赖性问题。实验表明，该工具包能捕捉CNN中几何度量无法识别的功能偏移，并在距离饱和条件下稳健映射LLM谱系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成对拓扑散度（如RTD）存在两个核心问题：启发式不对称性导致的方向不一致，以及更关键的——分数无界且依赖于样本量，阻碍了跨场景的可靠基准测试。需要一种既能提供细粒度结构诊断又能实现标准化评估的统一拓扑工具。

Method: 1) 提出对称表示拓扑散度（SRTD），通过整合双方向诊断信息为单一跨条形码签名，解决RTD的理论不对称性，并可作为优化目标。2) 提出归一化拓扑相似度（NTS），通过测量层次合并顺序的秩相关性，得到在[-1,1]范围内的尺度不变度量，克服未归一化散度的尺度与样本依赖问题。

Result: 实验表明：该工具包能捕捉CNN中几何度量无法识别的功能偏移；在距离饱和条件下稳健映射LLM谱系；作为优化目标有效且无需双重方向计算的开销。

Conclusion: 该工具包提供了严谨的拓扑感知视角，补充了CKA等现有度量方法，兼顾了细粒度结构诊断与鲁棒标准化评估的双重需求。

Abstract: Topological Data Analysis (TDA) offers a principled, intrinsic lens for comparing neural representations. However, existing paired topological divergences (e.g., RTD) are limited by heuristic asymmetry and, more critically, unbounded scores that depend on sample size, hindering reliable cross-scenario benchmarking. To address these challenges, we develop a unified topological toolkit serving two complementary needs: fine-grained structural diagnosis and robust, standardized evaluation. First, we complete the RTD framework by introducing Symmetric Representation Topology Divergence (SRTD) and its efficient variant SRTD-lite. Beyond resolving the theoretical asymmetry of prior variants, SRTD consolidates diagnostic information into a single, comprehensive cross-barcode signature. This allows for precise localization of structural discrepancies and serves as an effective optimization objective without the overhead of dual directional computations. Second, to enable reliable benchmarking across heterogeneous settings, we propose Normalized Topological Similarity (NTS). By measuring the rank correlation of hierarchical merge orders, NTS yields a scale-invariant metric bounded between -1 and 1, effectively overcoming the scale and sample-dependence of unnormalized divergences. Experiments across synthetic and real-world deep learning settings demonstrate that our toolkit captures functional shifts in CNNs missed by geometric measures and robustly maps LLM genealogy even under distance saturation, offering a rigorous, topology-aware perspective that complements measures like CKA.

</details>


### [251] [Function-Space Priors for Bayesian Neural ODEs with Application to Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06351)
*Jaeyeong Lee,Wonmo Koo,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合高斯过程核先验正则化与概率多步打靶法的贝叶斯神经常微分方程方法，用于AIS数据中不规则采样的船舶轨迹预测与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有船舶轨迹预测方法在不确定性校准方面不足，且贝叶斯神经常微分方程中常用的各向同性高斯权重先验无法编码船舶动力学的结构特性（如平滑性、局部性），而函数空间的先验方法难以直接应用于神经常微分方程中。

Method: 在向量场上施加基于高斯过程核的先验，通过核正则化项增强权重空间变分目标；结合概率多步打靶法将长序列解耦为时间片段进行推断，保持全局一致性。

Result: 该方法能够处理长序列和不规则采样的AIS轨迹，提供校准良好的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 提出的函数空间正则化与概率多步打靶法相结合的策略，有效提升了贝叶斯神经常微分方程在不规则船舶轨迹预测中的建模能力与不确定性量化质量。

Abstract: Vessel trajectory prediction from Automatic Identification System (AIS) data is essential for maritime situational awareness, yet it remains challenging due to irregular sampling, missing reports, and complex dynamics. Beyond accurate point forecasts, maritime applications also demand well-calibrated uncertainty estimates for reliable decision-making. Bayesian Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) offer a principled framework for continuous-time trajectory modeling with uncertainty quantification by placing a prior over the neural vector field parameters. However, the commonly used isotropic Gaussian weight prior fails to encode informative structural properties of vessel dynamics, such as smoothness and locality. Existing function-space Bayesian neural network methods address this limitation for static mappings, but do not transfer directly to Neural ODEs, where the primary quantity of interest is the trajectory rather than the vector field itself. In principle, one could place a Gaussian process (GP) prior directly over ODE solutions, but this requires propagating distributions through a nonlinear ODE solver, which is analytically intractable. To address this challenge, we adopt a practical approach that imposes a GP-kernel-based prior directly on the vector field evaluated at a finite set of measurement points. Specifically, we augment the standard weight-space variational objective with a kernel-based regularizer that penalizes deviations of the vector field from the structure implied by a GP prior. To handle long and irregular AIS trajectories, we further combine this function-space regularization with probabilistic multiple shooting, which decouples inference across temporal segments while maintaining global consistency.

</details>


### [252] [Conformal Risk Sharing: Certified Cost Allocation with Participation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06391)
*Ieva Kazlauskaite*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出认证分配问题（Certified Allocation Problem），并设计了Conformal Risk Sharing框架，通过可解释的分担策略与分裂保形校准，在无分布假设下为参与者提供义务上限保证，同时确保无人遭受实质性损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在群体中分担罕见不良事件的财务影响可以减轻极端个体负担，但任何因该安排而境况变差的参与者都有理由退出。因此需要一种可信机制，为每个主体提供未来义务的有保障上限，且仅在参与者总伤害有界时方可部署。

Method: 将问题形式化为认证分配问题；提出Conformal Risk Sharing框架，结合可解释的分担策略与分裂保形校准（split conformal calibration）；在训练数据上调整分担强度，在留出校准数据上生成分布无关的逐主体保证（在可交换性假设下有效）。

Result: 在合成数据与真实数据（包括降水和能源合作数据）上的实验表明，该框架能够显著降低高风险主体的极端义务，同时控制对其他主体的损害。

Conclusion: Conformal Risk Sharing有效解决了认证分配问题，在无需分布假设的前提下提供了可靠的义务上限保证，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Sharing the financial impact of rare adverse events across a group can soften extreme individual burdens, but any participant made worse off by the arrangement has reason to leave. A credible mechanism must therefore provide each agent with a trustworthy cap on their future obligation and should be deployed only if the aggregate harm across participants is bounded. We formalise this as the Certified Allocation Problem: from finite data and without distributional assumptions, find a redistribution rule, produce obligation caps for every participant, and verify that no participant is made materially worse off. We propose Conformal Risk Sharing, which solves this problem by pairing an interpretable sharing policy with split conformal calibration. The sharing intensity is tuned on training data, while held-out calibration data produces distribution-free per-agent guarantees (valid under exchangeability). Experiments on synthetic and real-world data, including precipitation and energy-cooperative data, confirm that the framework can substantially reduce extreme obligations for high-risk agents while controlling harm to others.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [Gradient descent at the Edge of Stability: free energy model and kinetic description of the two-layer network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05326)
*Antonin Chodron de Courcel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究梯度下降在"不稳定边缘"（Edge of Stability）阶段的动力学，提出了一个连续时间有效模型来追踪平均轨迹与快速振荡的时间平均协方差，并揭示了该阶段应监测的有效自由能（包含原始风险泛函和曲率相关"熵"项）。在宽两层神经网络中推导了新的平均场极限动力学方程（Wasserstein-2梯度流），并在矩阵分解和CIFAR-10深度学习任务上验证了模型捕捉振荡包络的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解梯度下降在Edge of Stability（大学习率导致损失和锐度持续振荡）这一不稳定阶段的动力学行为，跟踪振荡的包络线（包括训练中的"尖峰"现象），并建立能够描述平均轨迹与快速振荡耦合的理论框架。

Method: 提出连续时间有效模型：将平均轨迹与快速振荡的时间平均协方差耦合；引入有效自由能（原始风险+曲率相关熵项）；针对宽两层神经网络，推导平均场极限下的新型动力学方程（Wasserstein-2梯度流形式的宏观自由能）。

Result: 有效模型能够追踪振荡包络线，即使在平均动力学与振荡时间尺度相近时仍有效；在矩阵分解和CIFAR-10深度学习中验证了模型对振荡包络的捕捉精度和有效自由能的预测能力。

Conclusion: Edge of Stability阶段的动力学可由有效自由能理论描述，所提出的模型为理解大学习率训练中的不稳定振荡现象提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of gradient descent in the Edge of Stability regime, where the learning rate is large enough to induce persistent oscillations in the loss and the sharpness. We propose a continuous-time effective model that tracks the evolution of the average trajectory coupled with the time-averaged covariance of its fast oscillations. Our analysis reveals that the natural quantity to monitor in such unstable regimes is an effective free energy, which combines the original risk functional with a curvature-related "entropic" term. Our model allows us to track the envelope of the oscillations even in situations where its dynamics evolve on similar timescales as the averaged weights. Otherwise stated, we can track the spikes that occur during the training of some neural network architectures. For wide two-layer neural networks optimized under stable non-vanishing oscillations, we derive a mean-field limit that results in a novel kinetic equation describing the joint distribution of weights and their fluctuations. We show that this equation can be interpreted as a Wasserstein-2 gradient flow of a macroscopic free energy. Finally, we provide numerical evidence on matrix factorization and deep learning tasks (CIFAR-10) to demonstrate the model's accuracy in capturing the envelope of the oscillations and the predictive power of the effective free energy.

</details>


### [254] [Learning Implicit Feasibility Constraints for Real-World Routing and Scheduling: Application to Log Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05353)
*Abdelhakim Abdellaoui,Ayoub Boufous,Issmail El Hallaoui,Loubna Benabbou,Francois Aubé,Mouloud Amazouz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的约束学习方法，通过训练GNN、决策树、线性回归三个互补预测器，并采用堆叠机制将其嵌入MILP，在日志车路由调度问题中自动学习隐性操作规则，实现无需人工重新建模的约束适应，在工业数据上达到最优可行性-目标权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际车辆路由调度问题中，操作规则随时间动态演变（季节性/永久性），而传统MILP优化工具基于固定硬编码约束，更新需要建模与运筹学专业技能，规划人员难以胜任，导致方案需手工调整。因此需要一种能自动从历史数据中学习隐性约束的方法，使优化工具能适应规则演变而无需重复人工建模。

Method: 基于数据驱动的约束学习方法：在日志车路由调度问题(LTRSP)的历史执行数据上，训练三种互补预测器——图神经网络(GNN)、决策树、线性回归；通过线性化约束将每个预测器嵌入MILP；进一步提出堆叠机制，将三者组合成单一增强优化问题(AOP)，让求解器内生地选择每个决策最可靠的预测器。

Result: 在真实工业数据上，每个单独预测器均提升了可行性，但堆叠嵌入一致实现了最低的目标退化：(i)在未见实例上满足操作规则且退化小于任何单模型变体；(ii)无需预知规则性质即可为每个决策选择最合适的预测器；(iii)减少日常手工调整工作量，同时保持实际可解性。

Conclusion: 该框架使优化工具能够适应不断演变的实际操作规则而无需重复人工建模，为动态约束环境下的运筹优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-world vehicle routing and scheduling problems involve complex operational rules and feasibility constraints typically formulated as mixed-integer linear programs (MILP). However, optimization tools are built around a fixed set of hard-coded constraints, while in practice this set evolves as new rules or preferences emerge, seasonally or permanently. Updating it requires modeling and operations research skills that planners rarely have, so generated plans are routinely adjusted by hand based on practical knowledge. Building on recent work that uses machine learning to recover such hidden constraints, we propose a data-driven constraint-learning approach that trains three complementary predictors, a Graph Neural Network (GNN), a decision tree, and a linear regression, on historical execution data from a log-truck routing and scheduling problem ($\mathcal{LTRSP}$), and embeds each inside a MILP through linearized constraints. We further introduce a stacking mechanism that combines all three within a single augmented optimization problem (AOP), letting the solver endogenously select the most reliable predictor for each decision. On real-world industrial data, each predictor already improves feasibility, but the stacked embedding consistently achieves the lowest objective degradation: it (i)~satisfies the operational rules on unseen instances with smaller degradation than any single-model variant, (ii)~picks the most appropriate predictor per decision without prior knowledge of the rule's nature, and (iii)~reduces daily manual adjustment effort while remaining tractable for daily use. Beyond this application, the framework enables optimization tools that adapt to evolving practice without recurrent manual remodeling.

</details>


### [255] [Bayesian Equilibria of Heterogeneous Non-Atomic Routing Games with Private Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05424)
*Alexia Ambrogio,Leonardo Cianfanelli,Giacomo Como,Paolo Frasca*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究共享交通网络中协调车队与自私用户两类交通流的非原子贝叶斯路由博弈，证明在有限状态和消息集下贝叶斯均衡的存在性与唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实交通网络中同时存在受统一调度的车队和追求个人最优的 selfish 用户，且两者均基于不完全、异质信息（如私有信号）做路由决策，现有模型缺乏对此类混合信息结构的均衡分析。

Method: 建立非原子贝叶斯路由博弈模型，假设世界状态和私有消息集有限，协调者和自私用户分别观测到不同的私有消息（相互关联且与世界状态相关），基于先验分布和异质部分信息做路由决策，利用固定点理论证明贝均衡的存在性与唯一性。

Result: 在状态空间和消息空间有限的条件下，证明了该贝叶斯路由博弈存在唯一的贝叶斯均衡。

Conclusion: 该模型为分析存在信息异质性的混合交通流路由博弈提供了理论基础，均衡的唯一性确保了结果的可预测性和稳定性。

Abstract: We study non-atomic Bayesian routing games whereby a transportation network is shared by two types of traffic: a coordinated fleet and a mass of selfish users. The links in the network are characterized by travel time functions that depend both on the aggregate flow on the link and on a random state of the world $W$ that, in general, is not directly observable. Rather, we assume that both the fleet coordinator and the selfish users know the prior distribution of $W$, observe (different) private messages that are correlated with $W$ and possibly among themselves, and make routing decisions based on such heterogeneous partial information. Under the assumption that both the state of the world and the private message sets are finite, we prove the existence and uniqueness of a Bayesian equilibrium for the ensuing Bayesian routing game.

</details>


### [256] [Optimal experimental design for passive imaging source problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05490)
*Christian Aarset,Thorsten Hohage,Georg Stadler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对被动成像最优实验设计（OED）的两级低秩近似方法，有效解决了相关数据导致观测维度二次增长带来的计算瓶颈，实现了无需额外PDE求解的高效设计目标评估，数值结果表明该方法在大规模问题中效率优异且显著优于随机传感器布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动成像中相关数据的使用导致观测维度随传感器数量二次增长，使得最优设计算法的计算复杂度急剧增加，传统的PDE重复求解方法难以扩展到大规模问题。

Method: 采用贝叶斯逆问题框架，针对Helmholtz方程控制的空间非相关源问题，开发了两级低秩近似方法对A-最优设计目标进行近似，将问题解耦为离线阶段和在线阶段，实现无需额外PDE求解即可高效评估设计目标及其梯度。

Result: 所提算法能够高效扩展到大规模问题，得到的最优设计在最小化后验不确定性方面显著优于随机传感器布局。

Conclusion: 两级低秩近似方法有效克服了被动成像OED的计算瓶颈，为大规模被动源成像问题提供了可行的最优传感器设计策略。

Abstract: This work focuses on optimal experimental design (OED) methods for passive imaging. We adopt a Bayesian inverse problem framework for passive imaging source problems, primarily focusing on spatially uncorrelated sources and systems governed by the Helmholtz equation. A major challenge in passive imaging is that the use of correlation data causes the observation dimension to grow quadratically with the number of sensor locations, compounding the computational difficulty of finding optimal designs. To overcome the computational bottleneck of repeated PDE solves in optimal design algorithms, we develop a two-level, low-rank approximation of the A-optimal design objective. This effectively decouples the problem into an offline and an online phase, enabling efficient evaluation of the design objective and its gradient without additional PDE solves. Our numerical results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm efficiently scales to large problems and that the resulting optimal designs significantly outperform random sensor placements in minimizing posterior uncertainty.

</details>


### [257] [On Parallel and Batch-Cutting Strategies for Norm-Minimization-Based Convex Vector Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05617)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并行化和批处理切割策略，改进基于范数最小化的凸向量优化外近似算法。并行化将子问题分配到多个工作线程，减少每次迭代的墙钟时间；批处理切割利用所有已解子问题的信息，每次迭代添加最多K个支撑半空间。实验表明，8核并行加速1.1~4.2倍，批处理切割减少迭代次数62~80%，但墙钟时间收益因问题而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准外近似算法每次迭代需求解N_k个独立子问题以评估当前多面体近似的所有顶点，但仅处理单个最优切割，计算效率低下。

Method: 1) 将子问题评估并行化到n_w个工作线程，减少每次迭代的墙钟时间；2) 引入批处理切割策略，利用所有已解子问题的信息，每次迭代添加最多K个支撑半空间，而非仅用单个最优切割。

Result: 在8个测试问题（目标数q∈{2,3,4,5}）上，8核并行加速比为1.1~4.2倍；批处理切割持续减少迭代次数62~80%。

Conclusion: 批处理切割变体继承了标准算法O(k^{2/(1-q)})的收敛率。批处理切割的墙钟时间收益取决于问题特性：当每个顶点的子问题计算成本占主导时效果最佳。

Abstract: We develop parallel and batch-cutting variants of the norm-minimization-based outer approximation algorithm for convex vector optimization.
